[45] Venus-DefakerOne2: A Vision-Forensics Foundation Model with Broader Coverage cs.CVPDF

Chenfan Qu, Yachao Liang, Mingqi Fang, Jiangwei Xie, Zhenming Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉取证基础模型 Venus-DefakerOne2，旨在统一覆盖数字篡改与物理欺骗、图像与视频等多类伪造威胁。模型基于数千万开源与自建样本训练，采用 Tide-Style-Tuning 和基于熵的测试时自适应，在多个取证基准上取得领先表现。

Details

Motivation: 现实伪造形式异构，既包括数字操纵也包括物理欺骗，且媒介从图像扩展到视频；现有方法虽多，但缺少覆盖如此广泛威胁谱系的统一反伪造基础模型。

Result: 在 DeepFake 人脸、AI生成图像、篡改文档、自然图像操纵、物理欺骗和AI生成视频等基准上分别达到96.7%、93.3%、87.6%、89.6%、93.1%和96.0%，取得新的SOTA，并优于专用专家模型和前代 DefakerOne。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新在于沿伪造形式和媒体类型两个维度扩展取证能力，并用 Tide-Style-Tuning 交替 LoRA 与全参数微调以兼顾效率、泛化和难样本拟合、缓解灾难性遗忘；同时用预测熵做测试时自适应选择最佳预测模态。客观看，其价值主要在于统一多任务/多媒介取证框架与大规模训练下的工程化适配策略。

Abstract: Real-world forgery is inherently heterogeneous: it manifests not only as digital manipulation but also as physical spoofing, and is carried not only by images but also by videos. Despite substantial progress, a unified anti-forgery foundation model covering such a broad threat spectrum remains absent. In this paper, we present Venus-DefakerOne2, which substantially extends the forensic capability boundary along both the forgery-form and media-type dimensions. Trained on tens of millions of open-source and in-house samples, it incorporates two key designs. Tide-Style-Tuning alternates LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning to balance computational efficiency, generalization, and hard-sample fitting while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Entropy-based Test Adaptation determines the optimal prediction modality by analyzing prediction entropy. Venus-DefakerOne2 achieves new state-of-the-art performance across a comprehensive suite of benchmarks, including DeepFake faces (96.7%), AI-generated images (93.3%), tampered documents (87.6%), manipulated natural images (89.6%), physical spoofs (93.1%), and AI-generated videos (96.0%). It significantly outperforms both specialized expert models and its predecessor, DefakerOne, establishing itself as a powerful and versatile foundation model for binary-decision forensic tasks.

[46] You Don’t Need Aligned Representations: Knowledge Distillation via Random Prototype Spaces cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Hamza Etcibasi, Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis

TL;DR: 论文提出RPKD（随机原型知识蒸馏），放弃教师与学生表示必须对齐到兼容特征空间的常见假设，转而将两者与共享的随机初始化且冻结的原型向量库比较，在外部参考空间中完成对齐。教师侧仅用无参数的固定随机映射投影，只有学生侧适配；方法包含对齐全局原型相似度分布的逻辑分支和匹配空间原型激活图的特征分支。该方法在多个图像分类蒸馏基准上取得显著提升。

Details

Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏通常假设教师和学生表示需要显式对齐或处于兼容特征空间，论文希望去除这一限制，并在标签空间受限的低类别场景中提供比单纯标签空间更丰富的监督信号。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上相对OFA最大提升+7.98%，ImageNet-100上提升+12.44%，ImageNet-1K上提升+1.28%，实验验证了RPKD的有效性。

Insight: 创新点在于用共享的随机冻结原型库构建外部参考空间，教师侧采用无训练参数的固定随机投影，使学生仅在外部空间中适配；该设计可跨教师-学生架构复用原型库和蒸馏目标，并在低类别设置下通过解耦原型词表与任务标签空间获得更丰富监督。

Abstract: A widely held assumption in knowledge distillation is that teacher and student representations must occupy a compatible or explicitly aligned feature space. We propose RPKD (Random Prototype Knowledge Distillation), which discards this assumption by comparing both networks against a shared bank of randomly initialized, frozen prototype vectors. Alignment is therefore carried out in an external reference space rather than in the teacher’s native feature coordinates: the teacher side is projected by fixed random maps that carry no trainable parameters, and only the student adapts. Two branches handle the transfer: a logit branch aligning global prototype similarity distributions and a feature branch matching spatially-resolved prototype activation maps. Both operate within the same fixed prototype space, which can be interpreted as a random feature embedding that approximately preserves inter-sample similarity structure, so the prototype bank and the distillation objective stay unchanged across teacher-student pairs while only the input projections are instantiated per architecture. In low-category settings, decoupling the prototype vocabulary from the task label space yields richer supervision than the label space alone, an advantage absent from logit-based methods, whose supervisory signal collapses as class count shrinks. This advantage is especially relevant in real-world scenarios where the label space is typically constrained. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of RPKD, achieving a maximum gain of +7.98% over OFA on CIFAR-100, +12.44% on ImageNet-100, and +1.28% on ImageNet-1K. Source code is available at https://github.com/HamzaEtcibasi/RPKD

[47] Hierarchical Diffusion for Camera-controlled Autoregressive Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Kaihua Chen, Yiming Gong, Deva Ramanan, Tarasha Khurana

TL;DR: 论文面向单图像条件下的长视频相机控制生成，提出将自回归视频生成WanAR与分层长上下文去噪FixAR结合的轻量框架；通过warping-and-inpainting引入相机几何约束，并采用由近及远、由细到粗的分层去噪缓解自回归漂移。最终在RealEstate10k和DL3DV上实现时间稳定且符合输入相机轨迹的长视频生成。

Details

Motivation: 解决自回归长视频生成中的误差累积与时间漂移问题，以及相机控制难以有效融入生成过程的问题；现有self-forcing或显式6-DOF条件方法存在计算开销大或场景几何漂移的局限。

Result: 在RealEstate10k和DL3DV上，WanAR+FixAR优于先前相机控制长视频生成方法，生成结果时间稳定并与输入相机轨迹一致；摘要未给出具体定量指标。

Insight: 创新点在于将视频生成重构为warping-and-inpainting以施加相机几何一致性，并设计分层长上下文去噪：先基于短历史生成，再用时间下采样的长程历史精炼，以较低开销获得全局时间一致性。客观看，可取之处是分层条件机制在计算成本与长时一致性之间取得折中，并将前馈新视角合成思想与自回归视频生成结合。

Abstract: We address the task of long-video, camera-controlled generation from a single image. The dominant autoregressive paradigm suffers from two key limitations: (1) error accumulation over time, as distant frames are conditioned on previously generated outputs rather than ground truth, leading to drift; and (2) ambiguity in how to incorporate camera control. Standard solutions to both–such as self-forcing and conditioning on explicit 6-DOF poses–either incur significant computational overhead or produce drifting scene geometries over time. We propose a simple and lightweight framework that combines autoregressive generation (WanAR) with hierarchical long-context denoising (FixAR). To enforce scene geometry remains consistent with input cameras, we draw from feed-forward novel-view synthesis and reformulate video generation as a warping-and-inpainting problem. To mitigate drift, we introduce a hierarchical denoising strategy in which different stages condition on different temporal histories. Specifically, we first generate frames conditioned on the immediate past, and then refine them using a second denoiser conditioned on a temporally subsampled long-range history to enforce global consistency. This fine-to-coarse scheme enables each generated frame to effectively condition on the full temporal context without incurring a prohibitive cost. Experiments on RealEstate10k and DL3DV demonstrate that WanAR+FixAR outperforms prior methods for camera-controlled long-video generation, producing results that are both temporally stable and consistent with input camera trajectories.

[48] Where Can We Fool the Model’s Mind? Response-Guided Semantic Probing and Adaptive Routing for Transferable Attacks on Black-Box MLLMs cs.CVPDF

Ahmed Abdelnaby, Mohamed Elmahallawy

TL;DR: 论文提出VISTA黑盒框架，利用MLLM的响应构建不确定性多模态行为图，通过熵自适应Sinkhorn路由和替代集成聚焦语义脆弱区域，支持纯迁移（VISTA-Proxy）和查询辅助（VISTA-Victim）两种攻击设置，在多个商业MLLM上实现高攻击成功率且扰动隐蔽。

Details

Motivation: 现有黑盒攻击依赖替代模型显著性分配扰动，可能无法反映未知MLLM的语义解释或依赖区域，因此需要一种从模型响应中识别语义脆弱区域的可迁移攻击方法。

Result: 在GPT-5、Gemini-3.5-Flash和Claude Sonnet 4.6上，VISTA最高达到97%攻击成功率，同时PSNR保持在30dB以上；VISTA-Proxy无需查询最终受害者即可达到最高93%的ASR。

Insight: 创新点在于从响应漂移、冲突、误导和语义偏移中构建UBMB图，并结合熵自适应Sinkhorn路由与替代集成，在不访问梯度、logits、参数或内部表示的情况下实现有效的语义级迁移攻击。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) ground language in visual evidence, yet their semantic reasoning can be exploited through transferable black-box adversarial attacks. Existing attacks typically allocate perturbations using surrogate-model saliency, which may not reflect how an unseen MLLM semantically interprets or relies on different image regions. We introduce VISTA (Vulnerability-Informed Semantic Transfer Attacks), a black-box framework that identifies semantically vulnerable visual regions from an MLLM’s responses without access to gradients, logits, parameters, or internal representations. VISTA supports two attack settings: (i) VISTA-Proxy, a pure-transfer setting that probes an open-source MLLM and transfers the resulting adversarial examples to unseen commercial MLLMs without querying them; and (ii) VISTA-Victim, a query-assisted setting that probes the target MLLM to capture model-specific semantic vulnerabilities. VISTA builds an Uncertainty-Based Multimodal Behavior (UBMB) map from response drift, conflict, misleading, and semantic shifts, then uses entropy-adaptive Sinkhorn routing and a surrogate ensemble to focus perturbations on vulnerable regions. Across GPT-5, Gemini-3.5-Flash, and Claude Sonnet 4.6, VISTA achieves up to 97 % attack success rate (ASR) while maintaining a Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) above 30 dB. Notably, VISTA-Proxy achieves up to 93 % ASR without querying the final victim, demonstrating that response-derived semantic vulnerability can enable effective transfer across heterogeneous MLLMs.

Details

Motivation: 网球统计依赖弹跳和击球事件，而二者在单目视频中容易混淆。现有事件检测器多在广播视频上训练，论文关注其能否泛化到手机、固定高机和场边低视角等非广播相机。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于发布跨视角网球事件基准与评测协议，包括混淆感知匹配器、冻结预测和盲标注，并系统揭示广播相机检测器的跨视角失效。客观来看，RallyArb利用球轨迹和球员姿态进行无需事件标签参数的仲裁，以及留一片段微调分析，为跨视角事件检测提供了可复现的诊断思路。

Abstract: Ball bounces and racket hits are the events from which tennis statistics are derived, and on monocular video the two are easy to confuse. We release a benchmark of 345 bounce and hit annotations at frame level on eight clips that mix broadcast, fixed elevated, phone and court-level viewpoints, with a frozen development and test split, a confusion-aware matcher, clip-level bootstrap intervals and a blind second annotation of three clips. On it we score supervised precise spotters with published broadcast weights, two zero-shot vision-language models, and RallyArb, an arbitration layer over a ball track and player poses that fits no parameter on event labels. The supervised spotters score near-perfectly on broadcast footage and almost nothing on phone and court-level footage, where the pooled bounce F1 of E2E-Spot is 0.130. RallyArb keeps a bounce F1 above 0.8 on the three phone and court-level clips, two of which were used to tune it, and finds 8 of 11 bounces on the held-out one, but it places hits too far from the contact frame to match human timing. The vision-language models detect events on every viewpoint and miss the contact frame by about 0.1 s. Fine-tuned leave-one-clip-out from its broadcast weights, the spotter reaches about 0.8 bounce F1 on two phone and court-level clips and stays at 0.2 on a grazing phone angle. The annotations, the evaluator and the frozen predictions of every scored row are released.

Abstract: Traditional computed tomography (CT) can capture paired 3D face and skull geometry, but it remains costly, fixed in location, and exposes patients to radiation. For applications such as remote diagnostics, digital healthcare, forensic identification, missing-person investigation, and archaeological analysis, there is an urgent need for a portable, safe, and accessible solution to capture 3D skull geometry. In this work, we introduce Vision Tomography (VT), a concept and framework capable of reconstructing paired 3D face-skull geometry from various visual modalities, and also enabling joint manipulation of 3D facial and skull geometry. To achieve this, we first construct a dataset of over 200 samples, where each case includes a CT-based skull, a CT-based face, and a high-fidelity textured face scan. Secondly, we propose a novel dense ray matching registration method that ensures topological consistency across face, skull, and their tissue correspondences. Building on this registration, we introduce the 3D Bidirectional Face-Skull Morphable Model (BFSM) as the foundation of Vision Tomography, which enables shape inference between the face and skull through a shared coefficient space, while also modeling tissue thickness variation to support one-to-many facial reconstructions from the same skull, reflecting individual facial changes such as age or body fat. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of Vision Tomography across various forensic and medical applications, including paired 3D face-skull reconstruction from images or sketches and predicting surgical outcomes with joint face-skull editing. Extensive experiments confirm the robustness and accuracy of our method. Vision Tomography is available at https://github.com/wang-zidu/BFSM

[52] The Prestige: Benchmarking Cognitive Visual Reasoning using Magic Tricks cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Shuo Wen, Beixi Du, Edwin Meriaux, Junming Shi, Chloe Si

TL;DR: 论文提出 The Prestige，一个基于专业魔术表演的视频理解基准，通过故意误导来考察视觉语言模型的认知视觉推理能力，要求模型跟踪事件并解释意外结果。基准包含从时序与因果理解到对抗性问题鲁棒性的开放式问题，并系统评估了领先开源与专有 VLM，揭示其与人类表现差距、评分差异及失败模式。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频理解评测难以充分检验模型在误导性场景下的认知推理能力，论文希望解决模型能否在魔术误导中正确跟踪事件、理解因果并解释异常结果的问题。

Result: 在 The Prestige 基准上，最强 VLM 仍与人类表现存在显著差距；研究还发现人工与自动评分存在系统性差异，并识别出模型反复出现的失败模式。消融显示模型即使无法独立生成正确解释，也可能选对多项选择答案。

Insight: 创新点在于用专业魔术作为认知视觉推理测试场景，结合开放式解释与对抗性问题评估 VLM。客观来看，其价值还包括揭示开放式解释评估的可靠性问题，以及多项选择与自由解释之间的能力差异，为后续基准设计和问题格式研究提供启示。

Abstract: We introduce The Prestige, a video understanding benchmark that probes cognitive visual reasoning through professional magic performances, where deliberate misdirection challenges models to track events and explain unexpected outcomes. The benchmark comprises open-ended questions ranging from temporal and causal understanding to robustness against adversarial questions. Evaluation of leading open-source and proprietary VLMs reveals a substantial gap between even the strongest model and human performance. We also find systematic discrepancies between human and automated grading and identify recurring model failure modes. Ablations further show that models can select correct multiple-choice answers even when they fail to generate correct explanations independently, motivating further investigations into question formats. Together, these findings highlight limitations in both VLM reasoning and its evaluation, underscoring the need for more reliable video understanding models and more rigorous benchmarks.

[53] REEL: Same Frames, Different Answers and Different Costs cs.CVPDF

Xin Liang, Jiangpeng He

TL;DR: 论文提出REEL评估框架，用于评测「看一遍视频、建一个记忆、之后用记忆回答多个问题」的视频理解系统。REEL在完全相同的观测帧上构建三种记忆表示（Text文本记录、Visual编码器输出、Prefix State语言模型前缀），并与完整重处理（Frames）对比，通过配对正确性变化、累计GPU时间和存储大小来衡量差异。

Details

Motivation: 视频越来越长、可提的问题越来越多，逐题重处理不可扩展，因此系统倾向于预先建立记忆；但「记什么」在问题未知时就已决定，遗漏的信息无法找回。另外，用token数衡量效率会误导，因为token减少不代表模型复用已计算内容带来的节省。

Result: 在6个视觉语言模型和2个基准上，Text与Frames在20%~29%的问题上正确性不同，即便平均准确率几乎相同；Visual和Prefix State则更接近Frames。在存储不受限时，Prefix State在几乎保留Frames全部答对问题的记忆方案中总GPU时间最省，代价是每视频最多约21 GiB存储，而Text仅需KB级。

Insight: 创新点在于用同一观测帧做受控对比，把答案变化归因于记忆内容本身，并用配对正确性而非平均准确率来度量；同时指出「复用计算」与「丢弃信息」看似都是效率优化，实则本质不同，而平均准确率和token数都无法区分二者。

Abstract: Video understanding keeps moving toward longer recordings, and the longer a recording is, the more questions can be asked about it. But processing it again for every question does not scale, so many systems watch the video once, build a memory, and answer later questions from it. Yet what to remember is decided before anyone knows what will be asked, and whatever is left out cannot be recovered later. Moreover, whether the savings pay off is judged by token counts, which fall when the input gets shorter but not when the model reuses what it has already computed. We present REEL, an evaluation framework that compares three saved representations built from exactly the same observed frames, so that a change in answers can come only from what the system remembered. Text writes a short record of what happened, Visual keeps the encoder outputs, and Prefix State also keeps the language-model prefix. Each is compared with full reprocessing (Frames) by paired correctness changes, cumulative GPU time, and storage size. Across six vision-language models and two benchmarks, Text and Frames differ in correctness on 20% to 29% of questions, even when their mean accuracy is almost the same. By contrast, Visual and Prefix State stay much closer to Frames. With unlimited storage, Prefix State is the cheapest in total GPU time among memories that keep almost all of the answers Frames gets right, for every model on both benchmarks. The trade-off is storage: Prefix State keeps up to about 21 GiB per video, whereas Text needs only kilobytes. Reusing computation and discarding information both look like efficiency, but only the second removes evidence that later questions may need, and neither mean accuracy nor token count tells them apart. REEL makes that difference measurable for any system that answers many questions from one saved memory.

[54] Complementing Text Based Moderation with Multimodal Grounding: Detecting Fraudulent Inducement Behaviors on Short Video Platforms cs.CVPDF

Xiaoyong Yu

TL;DR: 论文面向短视频平台上的网络金融欺诈诱导行为检测，构建了包含2,419个短视频（66.49小时）、3,194个标注事件（四类）的数据集，并提出OCR+MLLM框架：保留OCR文本信号以保证召回，同时让多模态大模型联合执行基于OCR的判别和时间定位，从而同时捕捉文本与非文本诱导线索并精确定位违规片段。

Details

Motivation: 短视频已成为网络金融黑灰产的主要渠道，点赞、关注、加私聊群、点外链等诱导常通过转瞬即逝的视觉线索传达。现有OCR+LLM流水线只用屏幕文本分类，看不到OCR无法观测的视觉诱导，且基于关键帧的定位无法恢复精确时间边界。

Result: 在自建数据集上，OCR+MLLM的定位显著优于关键帧OCR+LLM基线（mIoU 26.52 vs. 14.49），能检出纯文本流水线遗漏的非文本诱导，同时保持67.0%的macro recall。

Insight: 创新点在于将OCR的召回保障与MLLM的视觉理解及时间定位能力结合，实现文本与非文本诱导的统一检测和片段级定位；论文还系统研究了类别不平衡下RL定位的奖励设计，发现辅助奖励项易被reward hacking利用、更强的定位奖励反而损害类平衡检测，据此提出recall-weighted F2定位奖励配合类平衡重采样。

Abstract: Short video platforms have become a primary channel for black and gray industry operations in internet finance, where inducements such as prompts to like, follow, join private chat groups, or click external links frequently steer users toward fraud. Because such videos are short, dynamic, and often convey intent through fleeting visual cues rather than stable text, a moderation system must guarantee detection recall and localize the offending segment so reviewers inspect only the relevant clip. Existing OCR+LLM pipelines transcribe on-screen text frame by frame and classify the transcript, but exploit only textual information. Many inducements are conveyed through visual cues OCR cannot observe, and keyframe-based localization cannot recover precise temporal boundaries. We explore OCR+MLLM, which augments the text pipeline with a Multimodal Large Language Model, and compare three routes: OCR+LLM, OCR+MLLM, and a large zero-shot MLLM, along detection, latency, token consumption, and deployment complexity. OCR preserves the text signal that guarantees recall, while the MLLM jointly performs OCR-grounded discrimination and temporal grounding, localizing both textual and non-textual inducements. We construct a dataset of 2,419 internet finance short videos (66.49 hours) with 3,194 annotated events across four categories, and study reward design for RL-based grounding under class imbalance. We report two findings: auxiliary reward terms are exploited through reward hacking, and stronger localization rewards degrade class-balanced detection. We therefore design a recall-weighted F2 localization reward with class-balanced resampling. Experiments show OCR+MLLM localizes better than the keyframe OCR+LLM baseline (mIoU 26.52 vs. 14.49) and detects non-textual inducements that text-only pipelines miss, while maintaining 67.0 percent macro recall.

[55] Silhouette-Based Gaitomics for Frailty Assessment in Older Adults cs.CVPDF

Laura McDaniel, Ryan Roemmich, Crystal Szczesny, Peter Abadir, Rama Chellappa

TL;DR: 论文提出基于轮廓的“步态组学”（gaitomics）方法，用于老年人衰弱评估。研究从标准化轮廓和步态能量图中提取90个形状、时序变化、运动与纹理描述符，结合mRMR特征选择和多种分类器进行三分类及两两分类，并用SHAP解释预测。结果显示模型可将预测与可测量的步态特征关联，但prefrail与nonfrail区分仍较有限。

Details

Motivation: 希望利用步行视频中的身体轮廓信息，实现可解释的衰弱评估，并将模型预测与具体的身体形态和运动模式联系起来，解决传统衰弱评估依赖量表、视频模型可解释性不足的问题。

Result: 在66名老年人中，CatBoost取得最高的三分类平均micro-AUC为0.647±0.085；两两分类中，frail vs nonfrail为0.833±0.249，frail vs prefrail为0.765±0.168，prefrail vs nonfrail仅0.554±0.129，表明prefrail与nonfrail分离有限。

Insight: 创新点在于提出轮廓驱动的“步态组学”描述体系，将mRMR特征选择、嵌套交叉验证与SHAP归因结合，把衰弱预测映射到可解释的步态特征上；分析发现双侧轮廓差异是关键特征，且不同分类任务的特征优先级不同。客观看，其价值主要在于可解释视频评估框架，但样本量小、部分分类性能有限，仍需更大独立研究验证可靠性和临床意义。

Abstract: Walking offers a window into physical function that may support interpretable video-based frailty assessment. We developed silhouette-based “gaitomics” to connect frailty predictions with measurable patterns of body configuration and movement. In 66 older adults assessed using the Fried Frailty Phenotype, standardized silhouettes and gait energy images yielded 90 descriptors of shape, temporal variation, motion, and texture. Minimum redundancy maximum relevance selection using the mutual information difference criterion prioritized informative features while limiting redundancy. Each classifier was evaluated using nested stratified cross-validation with five outer and three inner folds at the participant level, with feature ranking and feature-count selection confined to training partitions. Shapley additive explanations examined contributions to held-out predictions. Bilateral silhouette difference led the aggregate feature-selection rankings for three-class classification and both comparisons involving frail participants. Attribution profiles differed across tasks, with texture and consecutive-frame silhouette differences prominent in three-class classification. Among classifier-specific evaluations, CatBoost achieved the highest mean three-class micro-AUC of 0.647 \pm 0.085. Leading pairwise mean micro-AUCs were 0.833 \pm 0.249 for frail versus nonfrail, 0.765 \pm 0.168 for frail versus prefrail, and 0.554 \pm 0.129 for prefrail versus nonfrail classification. These findings connect model predictions to explicit gait characteristics while revealing task-dependent feature priorities and limited separation of prefrail and nonfrail participants. The framework provides an interpretable basis for further investigation, with larger independent studies needed to establish reliability and clinical relevance.

[56] MEV: A Multi-Event Video Dataset for Long-Take Generation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Peiyuan Zhu, Shaoan Xie, Yifan Shen, Wen Tian, Zijian Li

TL;DR: 论文提出MEV，一个面向长镜头视频生成的多事件视频数据集，包含主体执行一系列不重叠事件的高质量真实视频，具有精确时间边界和细粒度过程标注。论文还设计了将视频分割为时间接地事件并生成全局与事件级结构化字幕的标注流水线，并在事件中心评估协议下通过微调实验验证其能改善长镜头多事件生成的过渡与内容连贯性。

Details

Motivation: 现有文本到视频数据集多限于单事件短片段或粗粒度字幕，对长镜头时序依赖的监督不足，导致描述多个事件时生成结果时序不一致。

Result: 在事件中心评估协议下进行多事件生成微调实验，使用时间接地事件条件后，长镜头事件过渡和内容连贯性得到改善；摘要未给出具体量化指标或SOTA对比。

Insight: 核心创新是以事件为中心对长视频进行离散化，将长镜头分解为原子事件单元以减少混杂因素，并支持时间局部条件建模；同时提供精确时间边界、细粒度过程标注以及全局和事件级结构化字幕的完整标注流水线。

Abstract: Current datasets for text-to-video generation are largely limited to short clips depicting a single event or coarse-grained captions. As a result, they provide limited supervision for modeling long-take temporal dependencies, often leading to temporally inconsistent generations when describing multiple events. In this paper, we introduce MEV, a multi-event video dataset designed for long-take video generation. MEV provides high-quality real-world videos where subjects can perform a sequence of distinct, non-overlapping events, featuring precise temporal boundaries and fine-grained procedural annotations. This event-centric discretization reduces confounding factors and enables models to support temporally localized conditioning and build long-take videos from atomic event units. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive annotation pipeline that segments videos into temporally grounded events, and generates structured captions at both the global level and the event level. In addition, under an event-centric evaluation protocol, we conduct multi-event generation experiments by fine-tuning on MEV with temporally grounded event conditioning, leading to improved long-take event transitions and more coherent content. We believe this work provides a publicly available resource for multi-event fine-tuning and event-centric evaluation, together with practical insights into learning from temporally structured video-text data.

[57] TreemapMix: Dirichlet-Controlled Multi-Image Augmentation for Probability and Ordinal Supervision cs.CVPDF

Ejafa Bassam, Konstantin Garbers, Yingsheng Geng, Dalton Jens, Jiaxu Qian

TL;DR: 论文提出 TreemapMix，一种由 Dirichlet 分布控制的多图混合数据增强方法：从 Dirichlet 分布采样多张源图像的权重，并用 treemap 划分将其映射为面积成比例的区域。所得区域面积既可作为软类别概率目标配合 soft cross-entropy 使用，也可转化为序数监督配合 Plackett-Luce 目标使用。实验表明该方法在 ImageNet-1K 上分别提升校准性和 Top-1 准确率，并具有迁移到检测与分割任务的潜力。

Details

Motivation: 现有混合样本增强多局限于两图混合或固定小布局，难以灵活控制源图像数量、可见面积比例及由此诱导的监督信号。论文希望实现可控的多图组合增强，并系统利用区域面积信息构造概率或序数监督。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 且训练配方匹配的条件下，TreemapMix-SCE 的校准误差显著低于所评估的混合样本基线，TreemapMix-PL 取得最强的 Top-1 准确率；目标检测和实例分割迁移实验表明其预训练特征在分类之外仍有价值。

Insight: 创新点在于用 Dirichlet 分布控制多图源权重，并通过 treemap 分区生成面积比例明确的混合区域，使同一区域面积信息可同时支持软概率匹配和 Plackett-Luce 秩匹配两种监督形式。客观看，其价值主要在于将多图混合的布局、权重和监督目标统一到可控框架中，提升了混合增强的可解释性与任务适配性。

Abstract: Data augmentation improves visual recognition by exposing models to synthetic training examples that encourage generalization beyond the observed data. Mixed-sample methods extend this idea by combining multiple images. However, many common approaches either mix only pairs of images or rely on fixed small layouts, limiting control over source count, visible area proportions, and induced supervision. We propose TreemapMix, a distribution-controlled multi-image augmentation that samples source weights from a Dirichlet distribution and assigns them to area-proportional regions using a treemap partition. The resulting known region areas support two complementary objectives. TreemapMix with soft cross-entropy (SCE) treats areas as soft class-probability targets, while TreemapMix with a Plackett-Luce (PL) objective converts areas into ordinal supervision. On ImageNet-1K under matched training recipes, TreemapMix-SCE achieves substantially lower calibration error than evaluated mixed-sample baselines, while TreemapMix-PL achieves the strongest Top-1 accuracy. Transfer experiments on object detection and instance segmentation suggest that TreemapMix-pretrained features remain useful beyond classification. These results show that controlled multi-image composition can expose the same region-area information as either probability-matching or rank-matching supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/Ejafa/treemapmix.

[58] ConvRoute-ViT: Convolutional Token Routing for Efficient Vision Transformers cs.CVPDF

Manish Das, Shiv Ram Dubey, Satish Kumar Singh

TL;DR: 论文提出 ConvRoute-ViT，一种面向高效 Vision Transformer 的卷积式 token 路由机制。该方法在选定层根据 class-token 注意力保留重要 patch token，并将其余 token 空间重排并压缩为卷积 summary token，从而缩短序列长度且尽量保留上下文信息；层次化路由可为深层构建更紧凑、判别性更强的表示。

Details

Motivation: ViT 的自注意力计算随 token 数二次增长，且标准 ViT 在各层对所有 patch token 同等对待，带来冗余计算并可能削弱对判别性目标区域的关注。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 和 TinyImageNet 上，ConvRoute-ViT 相比基础 ViT 在保持分类准确率的同时提升了计算效率。

Insight: 创新点在于用卷积路由进行 token 缩减：保留 class-token 注意力的关键 token，同时将次要 token 压缩为卷积 summary token 而非直接丢弃，并通过层次化路由构建深层紧凑表示。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong image recognition performance, but their self-attention increases quadratically with the number of input tokens and makes them computationally expensive. The standard implementation of ViT models treats all patch tokens with equal importance across every layer of the model. This uniform treatment introduces redundant computation and may reduce the model’s focus on the most discriminative object regions. In this paper, we propose ConvRoute-ViT, a convolutional token routing mechanism for efficient vision transformers that performs token reduction through convolutional routing. Our model preserves the most relevant patch tokens according to class-token attention at selected layers, while the remaining tokens are spatially reorganized and compressed into a compact set of convolutional summary tokens. This design reduces the sequence length without completely removing the contextual information of the less informative tokens. By applying the routing process hierarchically, the proposed framework constructs compact and discriminative representations for deeper layers. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet show that ConvRoute-ViT improves computational efficiency while maintaining classification accuracy compared with the base ViT.

[59] PulmoWorld: An Asynchronous Multimodal Medical World Model for Longitudinal Pneumonia Dynamics cs.CVPDF

Cheng Tang, Xinyu Zhang, Jiawei Chen, Minghui Zhang, Shaocong Wei

TL;DR: 论文提出PulmoWorld，一个面向纵向肺炎动态的异步多模态医学世界模型，将异步状态估计、递归模拟和证据约束规划结合。模型用历史感知滤波在观测到达时更新共享信念，并基于该信念递归预测临床指标与模态表示及未来预后；Cap-MBRL在授权动作集内搜索想象轨迹，仅在满足风险预算和预测成本边际时替换冻结回退。

Details

Motivation: 肺炎涉及呼吸、炎症和器官功能的动态变化，但多模态测量异步到达且常不完整，现有方法难以统一建模纵向状态演化并支持安全规划。

Result: 在MASS肺炎队列上，递归预测与任务训练序列模型具有竞争力，模拟未来状态保留预后信息；在MIMIC-IV-CXR和临床轨迹上分别训练后，预后判别优于最新值融合，锁定肺炎面板长期误差具竞争力；在五个MASS-Syn环境中，Cap-MBRL相对直接回退执行降低选择遗憾。

Insight: 创新点在于历史感知滤波将状态传播与观测驱动校正分离，实现异步多模态共享信念更新；递归预测临床值与模态表示；并以风险预算和预测成本边际约束世界模型规划，兼顾纵向医学动态建模与安全决策。

Abstract: Pneumonia changes across respiration, inflammation, and organ function, while the measurements that describe it arrive at different times and are often incomplete. We introduce PULMOWORLD, a world model for longitudinal pneumonia dynamics that connects asynchronous state estimation, recursive simulation, and evidence-constrained planning. History-aware filtering separates state propagation from observation-driven correction, so a measurement updates the shared belief only when it is observed. That belief supports recursive forecasts of clinical values and modality representations, and summaries of future prognosis. Cap-MBRL searches imagined continuations inside an authorized action set and replaces a frozen fallback only when a candidate meets the risk budget and a predicted-cost margin. On the pneumonia cohort MASS, recursive forecasts are competitive with task-trained sequence models, and simulated future states retain prognostic information. Trained separately on public MIMIC-IV-CXR chest radiographs and clinical trajectories, this model improves prognostic discrimination over latest-value fusion in development and is competitive on long-horizon error on the locked pneumonia panel. In five known-mechanism MASS-Syn environments, Cap-MBRL lowers selection regret relative to direct fallback execution.

[60] Don’t Ask the LLM: Visual Token Pruning with Spectral Structure and Semantic Relevance cs.CVPDF

Intaek Shin, Geuntaek Lim, Sungjune Park, Yukyung Choi

TL;DR: 论文提出 ST-Pruner（Spectral-Textual Pruner），一种免训练的 LLM 前视觉 token 选择框架，用于降低大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的长视觉 token 序列推理成本。方法结合与注意力无关的谱显著性和倒置的 patch-文本相似度，通过无参数乘法融合完成视觉 token 剪枝。实验表明其能在激进压缩下保持性能并显著加速推理。

Details

Motivation: LVLMs 将图像和视频表示为长视觉 token 序列，导致推理成本高；视觉 token 剪枝的关键在于选择信号及其可用时机。现有视觉侧 [CLS] 注意力在激进压缩下选择分散且偏向背景，而更强的查询条件化信号需在 LLM 内部大量计算后才可得，因此论文希望在咨询 LLM 之前完成结构连贯且查询感知的 token 选择。

Result: 在多种 LVLMs 上，ST-Pruner 仅使用至多 11.1% 的视觉 token 即可保留超过 95% 的全 token 性能，并在 LLaVA-NeXT 上实现 3.2 倍端到端加速。

Insight: 创新点在于将视觉 token 选择前移到 LLM 之前，并以免训练方式融合注意力无关的谱显著性与语义相关性（倒置 patch-文本相似度），通过无参数乘法融合实现结构连贯且查询感知的剪枝。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) represent images and videos as long visual-token sequences, substantially increasing inference costs. These costs motivate visual-token pruning, whose effectiveness depends on both what signal is used for selection and when that signal becomes available. Our analysis reveals complementary limitations: visual-side [CLS] attention produces scattered, background-biased selections under aggressive compression, while stronger query-conditioned signals become available after substantial computation within the large language model (LLM). This raises a natural question: can structurally coherent, query-aware token selection be performed before consulting the LLM? We therefore propose ST-Pruner (Spectral-Textual Pruner), a training-free framework for pre-LLM visual-token selection. It combines attention-independent spectral saliency with inverted patch-text similarity through parameter-free multiplicative fusion. Across diverse LVLMs, ST-Pruner retains over 95% of full-token performance using at most 11.1% of visual tokens and achieves a 3.2x end-to-end speedup on LLaVA-NeXT. Code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/ST-Pruner.

Abstract: Football action valuation estimates players’ contributions to future scoring. Existing multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL) models use 2D positions and velocities, omitting posture information relevant to imminent actions. We investigate whether adding 3D pose to a SARSA-based model improves observed-action values relative to attack returns: goals, conceded goals or final expected possession value. Using UEFA Champions League 2022/23 event, tracking and 3D pose data, we compare six representations (no pose, raw joints, torso and head orientation, and spatial-temporal graph convolutional network (ST-GCN) embeddings) across five learning settings and three seeds. The settings vary temporal-difference targets and action-prediction loss, with Monte Carlo return regression as a reference. Across 50 on-ball and off-ball comparisons, pose robustly improved return prediction in only three, all using torso and head orientation, while worsening it in twelve, mostly with ST-GCN. Separately, pose improved prediction of the on-ball action 0.2 s ahead (macro AUC 0.914 to 0.934 with raw joints), while the passer’s pose during execution improved pass-success prediction (AUC 0.844 to 0.856). Abstract: Extremely sparse-view computed tomography (xSV-CT) is critical when scanning time, motion, or radiation tolerance limits each scan to only a few projections. This exposes a gap in current sparse-view approaches: Optimization-based methods, which use the sparse measurements directly but lack data-driven priors to suppress artifacts, and learning-based methods, which use strong priors but usually require densely scanned training volumes acquired outside the xSV-CT regime. Abstract: While single-view novel view synthesis methods have been dramatically enhanced recently, how to quantify their performance has yet to be standardized. In this work, we demonstrate that an important factor lies in how each method’s own reference frame is aligned with the so-called ground-truth pose. Existing methods often rely on test-time optimization of target poses for this alignment, which inherently is ill-posed due to the complexity of the optimization landscape of camera poses vs render accuracy, leading to methods being evaluated at an arbitrary local optimum. Some recognize this limitation and thus rely on 3D reconstructions of entire scenes using training and test views to align poses, which may be infeasible. In this work, instead, we propose to use traditional 2D-to-2D and 2D-to-3D image matching techniques to align 3D reconstructions. We also evaluate only within co-visible regions, between different views, which is the only place where a `correct’ ground truth can be defined. Our evaluations show that with proper coordinate alignment, novel-view synthesis methods that use explicit 3D Gaussians achieve the state of the art, despite being previously reported in the literature to be outperformed by pure image-based 2D methods. Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) show strong performance in radiology report generation (RRG) and medical visual question answering (Med-VQA), producing free-form clinical descriptions and answers from medical images. However, they may generate clinically meaningful statements that lack visual evidence or contradict image content. We refer to these errors as visually irrelevant hallucinations and investigate them at the phrase level. We propose CLEAR (Counterfactual Localized Evaluation of Alignment and Response), a model-agnostic post-hoc framework for phrase-level hallucination risk estimation. CLEAR decomposes generated responses into visually assessable clinical phrases. For each phrase, phrase-conditioned grounding identifies a candidate visual region, which is selectively perturbed by a localized counterfactual intervention. CLEAR characterizes the intervention using two complementary auditing cues: response cues, capturing semantic and clinically meaningful changes in the generated response, and alignment cues, measuring changes in image-phrase compatibility. These cues are normalized and jointly modeled to estimate phrase-level hallucination risk. Across four datasets spanning RRG and Med-VQA, CLEAR outperforms state-of-the-art hallucination detection baselines across multiple medical and general-purpose VLMs. On MIMIC-CXR, CLEAR improves AUPRC from 85.10% to 90.23%. Its effectiveness is further validated on a GT-derived controlled VIH benchmark. These results establish visually irrelevant hallucination as an important fine-grained reliability failure mode in medical VLMs and demonstrate the value of model-agnostic visual-support auditing for more trustworthy multimodal clinical generation.

Abstract: Real-world deployment of computer vision models, particularly in outdoor scenarios, is often limited by environmental interferences, such as adverse weather. Improving model robustness across weather conditions is thus critical for reliability. However, sourcing high-quality data relevant to isolated weather effects is a significant challenge. Most datasets include changes in dynamic elements across observations of the same scene: subjects such as pedestrians and vehicles change between shots, while camera viewpoints vary. These aspects make it difficult to decouple solely weather-induced distribution shifts from the scene. In this work, we address this with a novel weather synthesis framework that utilizes structure-retaining Low-Rank Adapters (LoRA). Abstract: Humans routinely consume multimodal content, where textual and visual information is naturally interleaved. Abstract: The large-scale digitisation of medieval manuscripts creates a growing need for automated iconographic indexing. Abstract: Few-shot action recognition (FSAR) requires representations that expose discriminative motion dynamics from only a few labeled videos. Recent trajectory-based methods leverage long-term point tracks for explicit temporal correspondence, but often treat trajectories as carriers of sampled visual features or summarize their motion with compact 2D displacement-based descriptors. We instead view an action as the ordered evolution of 2.5D trajectory states and their local geometric interactions. Based on this view, we introduce GeoTRAM, a geometry-aware trajectory state modeling framework for FSAR. GeoTRAM lifts sparse tracks into 2.5D trajectories, where StateMotion models ordered intra-trajectory state evolution and RelMotion captures state-conditioned local interactions through time-varying relative geometry and motion. Quality-filtered spectral sampling provides more reliable trajectory initialization; complementary semantic, motion, and depth cues are separately refined and preserved for joint spatio-temporal modeling; action-guided reweighting and top-$ρ$ trajectory selection support complementary frame- and trajectory-level matching. Abstract: Traffic congestion is becoming a critical challenge in rapidly growing urban centers, particularly in developing nations where rapid urbanization and inadequate transport infrastructure place increasing strain on transport networks. In highly heterogeneous and chaotic traffic flows, camera-based traffic-state estimation is limited by severe vehicle occlusion and overlap. In this paper, we propose a framework to optimize traffic flow directly from stochastic visual observations. The architecture is structurally decoupled into three primary components: (a) vehicle density estimation, (b) predictive dynamics modeling, and (c) policy optimization. Abstract: Continuous space-time video super-resolution produces a high-resolution, highframe-rate video from a low-resolution, low-frame-rate one at arbitrary spatial scales and temporal instants. Classical spatial multi-frame super-resolution has shown that detail beyond a single frame’s sampling limit can be recovered by aligning complementary observations with sub-pixel precision and accumulating them on a finer grid. Existing learning-based methods largely depart from this principle: some align and fuse frames only at low resolution before regressing a continuous high-resolution representation, while others interpolate each frame to the output resolution before splatting. In both cases, the per-frame samples are resampled before fusion, so their sub-pixel positions are lost before frames are combined. Restoring this principle in a learned continuous model raises two challenges: estimating sub-pixel motion at unobserved times, and handling holes and occlusions when splatting a coarse grid onto a finer one. We address the first by predicting per-pixel continuous trajectories that refine linear optical-flow paths with smooth residual motion, using features integrated over the full video sequence. We address the second by directly splatting consecutive low-resolution features in a learned latent space onto a high-resolution grid at their predicted sub-pixel positions with predicted visibility weights, before a decoder resolves remaining ambiguities. This makes temporal interpolation and multi-frame superresolution a single operation. Our method improves both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality on multiple standard benchmarks over the state of the art, while running up to an order of magnitude faster.

Abstract: Video captioning models are increasingly evaluated with metrics designed for single-sentence image captions. Abstract: Detecting whether and in particular how two data distributions differ is a fundamental statistical problem. For example, we might be interested in which concepts (expressed in natural language) differ between two classes, the outputs of different generative models, or the same model with different prompt specifications. Result: 在多个数据集和多样天气条件下进行大量实验，所提方法产生更清晰、更准确的深度预测，并持续优于当前最先进方法。

Insight: 创新点在于将 SAM 的对象级先验系统性地融入自监督深度估计：通过分割引导表示对比增强特征对象感知，结合自适应区域异常值掩码与区域高斯似然损失促进区域平滑，并利用对象级可靠性估计降低不可靠监督影响。

Abstract: Robust depth estimation aims to maintain high-quality depths across diverse conditions. However, most existing methods estimate depth without taking into account the object-level information. As a result, the predicted depth may easily deviate within objects and become blurred under adverse conditions. To overcome this weakness, we propose RoSAMDepth, a novel framework that can assist robust self-supervised depth estimation in leveraging rich and diverse object-level priors from the Segment Anything Model (SAM). We focus on incorporating object-level information across three key aspects: a segment-guided representation contrasting method that injects object-level awareness into the feature representation space; an adaptive regional outlier masking strategy combined with a regional Gaussian likelihood loss that enforces regional depth smoothness; and an object-level reliability estimation strategy that mitigates the influence of unreliable supervision. Result: 论文在五个大规模数据集上进行域内训练，观察到随数据量增加从标准幂律转为饱和的两阶段缩放行为，且固定模型下临界转变点约保持不变；跨域性能对源域数据规模的依赖明显更弱且更难预测，分布与覆盖多样性更有利于缩小域差。

Abstract: In the era of large models, stereo matching is developing towards generalizable foundation models trained on million-scale synthetic images. However, how model performance scales up with synthetic data is still underexplored. In this work, we preliminarily investigate the in-domain and cross-domain data scaling laws in stereo matching. A realistic synthetic dataset, named XStereo, containing 200 thousand samples is first built to serve as a high-quality benchmark for data scaling research. In-domain training on five large-scale datasets demonstrate a two-regime scaling behavior with data, transitioning from the standard power law to saturation, where the critical transition point is approximately invariant under a fixed model. Cross-domain performance exhibits a substantially weaker and less predictable dependence on the source-domain data scale. The distribution and coverage diversity are more effective for diminishing domain gaps. More instructive suggestions are provided for high-efficiency data construction and training. The insightful findings are significant verification and extension of the existing scaling laws in both language and vision, paving the way to efficient and stronger stereo matching. The XStereo dataset is public at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tong-Zhao/XStereo.

Abstract: Recent advances in generative image editing enable complex transformations such as background replacement, object manipulation, and pose adjustment from natural language instructions. This challenge is particularly pronounced at scale, where manual inspection is infeasible and automated quality assessment (AutoQA) could become essential. In this work, we introduce Visual Consistency Assessment for Product Image Editing (V-CAPE), a large-scale dataset for evaluating visual consistency in generative image editing. Motivation: 现有主动交互范式假设回答所需信息完整，模型只能等待或回答，但现实中信息常因视频或用户不确定性而不完整，导致模型无限等待或产生幻觉，因此需要让模型主动询问缺失信息。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models have increased interest in interaction paradigms for video understanding. The evolution from offline to streaming and proactive interaction has gradually shifted models from passive responders toward more proactive agents that can decide when to answer. However, existing proactive paradigms typically assume that the information required to answer a query is complete, leaving models to only wait or answer. In the real world, however, information is often incomplete due to uncertainty from either the video or the user. In proactive paradigms, such uncertainty can cause the model to wait forever or generate highly hallucinatory responses. We therefore argue that proactive interaction should be redefined with a third action: ask, allowing models to actively ask for missing information. To study when and what models should ask before answering, we introduce AskStream, the first multi-turn benchmark spanning video and user uncertainty for streaming video understanding, with four task categories and eight subtasks. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended settings, even the strongest model attains only 52.18% on our proposed Ask-F1 metric, highlighting substantial limitations in proactive information seeking under uncertainty.

In this work, we introduce inkGround, a benchmark for stroke trajectory recovery from full-page, camera-captured handwriting. inkGround pairs 537 page images (~18.7k words, ~163k strokes) with reference trajectories produced by a pretrained offline-to-online model, filtered by OCR agreement, and verified by human raters. Abstract: Visual rule reasoning asks whether multimodal models can recover an abstract concept from a few examples. We present Bongard-Forge100k, a controlled benchmark, together with RuleForge, a backbone-agnostic framework that jointly learns visual rule understanding and rule-conditioned executable generation. TL;DR: 论文提出局部点跟踪（LPT）框架，用于视频喉镜中的声带定位。该方法采用“先定位、后吸附”流程：单帧语义检测器生成稀疏锚点并初始化局部查询点，点传播模块在检测器漏检帧中跟踪这些点并恢复时序边界框，随后通过box-snap细化边界。实验在两个数据集上验证了其整体性能和鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 视频喉镜中声带定位受组织快速形变、镜面高光和器械遮挡影响，现有基于区域或掩码记忆的方法易受时序误差累积和边界不紧问题困扰。论文旨在在检测器漏检和复杂视觉条件下实现稳定、边界准确的声带定位。

Result: 在两个不同数据集上，LPT在mAP_50、success AUC和missing rate三项指标上均取得最佳，整体优于现有基线。消融表明点跟踪主要提升时序跟踪鲁棒性，box-snap有效改善边界紧致度。

Insight: 创新点在于将时序状态保存在局部点坐标而非有状态掩码或全区域表示，并解耦时序跟踪与单帧边界细化，避免掩码误差反馈到时序记忆。客观来看，这种“点跟踪+边界吸附”的分解为遮挡、形变和漏检场景提供了更轻量鲁棒的定位思路。

Abstract: Reliable vocal-cord localization in emergency video laryngoscopy remains visually challenging due to rapid tissue deformation, specular highlights, and instrument occlusions. We propose Local Point Tracking (LPT), a robust framework that combines a single-frame detector, local point propagation, and stateless mask refinement. LPT follows a localize first, snap second pipeline. First, a single-frame semantic detector generates sparse anchors, within which LPT initializes local query points (SAM-supported by default, with a regular grid as fallback). A point-propagation module tracks these points through detector-missed frames and recovers temporal bounding boxes from their geometry. Second, because point-derived boxes can be loose around the visible boundary, a box-snap step refines the selected box to the target boundary. This decomposition keeps temporal state in local point coordinates rather than a stateful mask or whole-region representation, while the spatial refinement module handles single-frame boundary precision without feeding mask errors back into temporal memory. Extensive evaluations on two distinct datasets demonstrate that our proposed LPT method achieves strong overall performance against existing baselines, with the best mAP_50, success AUC, and missing rate on both datasets. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that point tracking primarily enhances temporal tracking robustness, while box-snap refinement effectively improves boundary tightness.

Result: 在12个SOTA开源权重和专有VLM上，互补视图下的计数准确率仅为4.5%-50.0%，而完整重叠样例为45.5%-81.5%；跨视图一致性在各模型上约为50%，包括较新的专有VLM，尽管空间推理表现明显更强。结果表明当前模型在处理多图和跨视图整合证据之间存在显著差距。

Insight: 创新点在于通过控制物体跨视图重叠、在共享区域与视角特定区域引入局部编辑、构造需要跨视角组合证据的空间关系，系统地区分“逐视图处理”与“场景级跨视图整合”。客观看，其价值在于提供了可诊断VLM多视图理解短板的受控评测框架，并揭示了一致性判断和实例级对应仍是薄弱环节。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly process multiple views of the same scene, but it remains unclear whether they integrate these observations into a coherent scene-level representation. Existing multi-image evaluations do not isolate this capability, as tasks may admit correct solutions through independent per-view processing without requiring cross-view correspondence. We introduce OneScene-Bench, a controlled benchmark designed to explicitly distinguish when cross-view integration is necessary. Our benchmark evaluates three complementary capabilities on real-world multi-view scenes: unique object counting, cross-view consistency, and spatial reasoning. For counting, we control object overlap across views to separate cases solvable by simple per-view aggregation from those requiring instance-level correspondence. For consistency, we introduce localized edits in shared and view-specific regions, testing whether models distinguish genuine scene contradictions from differences caused by viewpoint and visibility. For spatial reasoning, we construct object relations that require combining spatial evidence across viewpoints to compare physical proximity. We evaluate twelve state-of-the-art open-weight and proprietary VLMs and find that counting accuracy drops substantially on complementary views, ranging from 4.5-50.0%, compared with 45.5-81.5% on complete-overlap examples. Moreover, cross-view consistency remains around 50% across models, including recent proprietary VLMs, despite substantially stronger performance on spatial reasoning. Our results reveal a substantial gap between processing multiple images and integrating evidence across them, highlighting the need for evaluations that explicitly test scene-level multi-view understanding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/swathisuhas/OneScene-Bench.

In this work, we introduce MIMO-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs in a Multi-Instance per Multi-Object categories (MIMO) setting. Each image-question pair in our benchmark pertains to instances belonging to object categories with multiple instances. The presence of such similar feature distractors causes hindrance in basic visual reasoning tasks, necessitating the referring expression to be composed of multiple visual subtasks to uniquely and unambiguously identify the target object. MIMO-Bench comprises 3,802 image-question pairs across attribute binding and numerical counting tasks. Despite impressive performances on existing benchmarks, both SOTA open-source (InternVL, QwenVL) and proprietary (Gemini, GPT-5) models exhibit substantially lower performance on MIMO-Bench, highlighting their limitations in the MIMO setting. The benchmark will be made publicly available.

[85] AlbumVSR: Identity-Preserving Face Video Super-Resolution from a Personal Photo Album cs.CV | eess.IVPDF

Details

Motivation: 严重模糊、下采样、噪声和压缩会抹除人脸身份线索，仅依赖退化视频难以忠实恢复身份，因此需要借助同一人的照片相册提供额外身份信息。

Insight: 创新点在于将个人相册作为外部身份源，结合 set tokens 与基于估计人脸姿态渲染的 UV 纹理 tokens，并通过缓存 cross-attention 注入冻结流式扩散 Transformer，从而在视频超分中增强身份保持能力。

Abstract: Severe blur, downsampling, noise, and compression can erase the identity cues needed to faithfully restore a person’s face. We therefore use photographs of the same person as an external source of identity. AlbumVSR conditions a frozen, one-step streaming diffusion transformer on a personal album. We use four MagFace/CLIP set tokens to encode the album, and 64 texture tokens which come from a fused UV atlas rendered at the current window’s estimated face pose. These tokens are appended to the transformer’s cached cross-attention context. [86] Kinematic Causal Attribution: Black-Box Explanations of Motion versus Geometry for 4D Point-Cloud Video Models cs.CVPDF

Zerui Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出运动学因果归因（KCA），一种仅依赖模型输出的黑盒干预式归因方法，用于审计4D点云视频模型在分类时依赖区域运动还是几何形状。KCA通过冻结运动保持形状、以及去除形状保持运动的代理操作，构建运动×几何立方体，并用Shapley分解量化各因素贡献。作者在MSR-Action3D上对PSTNet、P4Transformer和Mamba4D进行验证，表明该方法能区分运动与形状线索且不损失归因忠实性。

Details

Motivation: 4D点云视频模型正被用于机器人、辅助监控和AR，但部署前缺乏工具检查模型依赖的是身体部位如何运动还是其形状。现有3D归因局限单帧、视频归因限于RGB像素、梯度法需白盒访问，且点级扰动假设跨帧点对应，难以适用于真实深度和LiDAR数据。

Result: 在MSR-Action3D上对官方PSTNet、P4Transformer和Mamba4D验证：运动审计任务中手臂重要性的运动占比随定义线索变化，分别为P4Transformer 0.83 vs. 0.60、PSTNet 0.86 vs. 0.30、Mamba4D 0.92 vs. 0.61。必要性图在P4Transformer和PSTNet上取得最低删除AUC，在Mamba4D上与最佳基线持平，且权重随机化后与训练模型的相关性消失。

Insight: 创新点在于用黑盒干预操作解耦运动与几何形状，构建运动×几何立方体并进行顺序对称的Shapley分解，同时显式测量交互作用而非假设其不存在。操作符直接重写每帧点集，无需跨帧点对应，context-averaged估计器可处理跨多个区域的结构，为4D点云视频模型提供可审计的因果归因。

Abstract: 4D perception models, which classify 3D point-cloud videos, are being built into robots, assistive monitoring and AR, yet before deploying one a practitioner cannot check which cue it relies on: how a body part moves, or what shape it has. 3D attribution is single-frame, video attribution is confined to RGB pixels, gradient maps need white-box access, attention is absent from state-space backbones, and point-level perturbations assume a point correspondence across frames that real depth and LiDAR data lack. We present Kinematic Causal Attribution (KCA), a black-box, interventional attribution method that queries only the model’s outputs. Its freeze operator removes a region’s motion while keeping its shape, and a shape-free proxy removes the shape while keeping the motion. Together they span a motion$\times$geometry cube for every tracked region, whose order-symmetric Shapley decomposition is complete and whose interaction is measured rather than assumed away. The operators rewrite per-frame point sets, so they need no correspondences, and a context-averaged estimator handles structures that span several regions. We validate KCA on the official PSTNet, P4Transformer and Mamba4D trained on MSR-Action3D. The decisive test is an audit: two binary tasks are built from real clips whose classes differ only in the motion or only in the shape of the same arm, and the motion share of the arm’s importance follows the defining cue on all three backbones (0.83 vs. 0.60 on P4Transformer, 0.86 vs. 0.30 on PSTNet, 0.92 vs. 0.61 on Mamba4D), a distinction no single attribution map can make. The split costs no faithfulness: the necessity maps have the lowest deletion AUC on P4Transformer and PSTNet, tie with the best baseline on Mamba4D, and lose their correlation with the trained model’s once the weights are randomised. Skeleton ground truth, re-sampling stability and cost complete the validation.

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables joint characterization of tissue morphology and gene expression. However, 3D volumetric ST acquisition remains expensive. AI-driven methods offer a low-cost alternative but remain limited in accuracy, while recent 2D section-wise ST acquisition still rely on dense grid sampling, resulting in redundant measurements and undersampling of biologically informative regions. To bridge the gap, we propose STRATA, a reinforcement-driven active sampling framework with tri-paradigm adaptation for reconstructing dense 3D transcriptomic maps from sparse voxel-wise ST acquisition. STRATA constructs a 3D graph integrating H&E morphology with single-cell priors and employs a reinforcement learning agent to estimate morphological sparsity and transcriptomic informativeness, enabling budget-aware selection of informative spots. Given these sparse measurements, an adaptive tri-paradigm predictor combines retrieval, regression, and cross-layer imputation through lightweight fusion, dynamically weighting their contributions. Experiments across datasets demonstrate improved sampling efficiency and accuracy, particularly under low-budget settings. Code is available at https://github.com/Junchao-Zhu/STRATA.

Details

Motivation: 现有 agentic 视觉生成流程多停留在反复试错的浅层循环，VLM 反馈较粗粒度，修复经验易丢失或无门控更新污染技能库，难以实现持续自演化。论文希望解决密集用户约束下视觉生成/编辑的可靠修复与经验积累问题。

Abstract: The central bottleneck in visual generation and editing has migrated from achieving pixel-level fidelity to satisfying densely constrained user specifications through increasingly agentic workflows. Prevailing agentic generation pipelines remain shallow generate-verify-rewrite loops treating VLM judgments as coarse visual feedback, either discarding repair experience or admitting ungated updates that poison skill library. With memorize and govern periods left open, they degrade into ungoverned iterators of repeated trial-and-error rather than continual self-evolving harness. Towards this issue, we present \textbf{VEvo-Harness}, the first causally-guarded continual self-evolving harness system for agentic visual generation. VEvo-Harness introduces four coupled innovations: (1) \textit{a visual traceback} that densifies sparse judgments into root-cause attributions with a hierarchical verification tree; (2) \textit{a visual continual governance} that admits only causally valid skill patches through counterfactual dual-arm image generation replay; (3) \textit{a lifecycle-managed harness state} that hierarchically distills root-cause-anchored episodes across working, experience, and pattern tiers into a persistent skill library of routable and causally verified capabilities; (4) \textit{a heterogeneous sandbox policy} that orchestrates a diverse action space of repair primitives, dynamically routing cost-aware interventions based on root causes to select the most effective strategy. VEvo-Harness surpasses state-of-the-art agentic methods and rivals proprietary generators (\textit{e.g.}, GPT-Image, Nano-Pro) while maintaining the continual harness evolution and generalization.

Sihyun Kim, Sanghyun Jo, Kyungsu Kim

TL;DR: 论文研究以编辑后的端点图像作为图像到视频生成的目标时，全帧端点条件会把源图保留的布局上下文误当成视频最终状态约束，从而产生时序伪影。作者提出 Partial 选择性条件方案，仅约束由图像编辑器注意力估计出的编辑相关区域，其余末端内容交给视频模型生成，并在多种视频模型和 TC-Bench-I2V 上验证其能减少时序伪影且不明显降低过渡完成率。

Details

Motivation: 编辑端点图像能明确图像到视频过渡目标并提升过渡完成度，但也会保留源图上下文；全帧端点条件将这些保留上下文转化为对视频最终状态的要求，导致硬切、背景运动反转和运动抑制等时序伪影。论文旨在解决如何利用编辑端点作为目标而不对非编辑区域施加过度约束的问题。

Result: 在 TC-Bench-I2V 上，Partial 在三种骨干模型上相对全帧条件降低时序伪影，且 Transition Completion Ratio 无显著差异；在 Wan2.2-Turbo 上，均匀弱化条件不能复现这些收益。Donor-context 干预进一步显示，仅替换编辑相关区域外的上下文即可在 HunyuanVideo-1.5 和 Wan2.2-Turbo 上降低 HC/BMR，并在 Wan2.2-Turbo 上恢复 DD，同时 TCR 无显著差异。

Insight: 创新点在于将编辑端点视为“部分未来目标”，只对编辑相关区域做条件约束，并用图像编辑器注意力估计该区域；同时引入 HC、BMR、DD 等时序伪影度量和 donor-context 干预来区分约束范围与约束内容的影响。客观来看，其价值在于提出选择性端点条件和归因实验，但也指出编辑相关性并不保证未来有效性。

Abstract: Edited endpoint images make the target of an image-to-video transition explicit and can improve transition completion, but also preserve context in the source image’s layout. Full-frame endpoint conditioning turns this preserved context into requirements on the video’s final state, potentially inducing temporal artifacts. We measure discontinuity with Hard-Cut (HC), late background reversal with Background Motion Reversal (BMR), and motion suppression with Dynamic Degree (DD). These artifacts appear across five video models spanning training-free and learned endpoint conditioning, in model-specific forms. We study Partial, a selective conditioning scheme that constrains only an edit-relevant region estimated from image-editor attention and leaves the remaining terminal content to the video model. Across three backbones on TC-Bench-I2V, Partial reduces temporal artifacts relative to full-frame conditioning, with no significant difference in Transition Completion Ratio (TCR); on Wan2.2-Turbo, uniformly weakening conditioning does not reproduce these reductions. A donor-context intervention keeps full-frame conditioning and replaces only the context outside the edit-relevant region with the final frame of a Partial video. It lowers HC and BMR on HunyuanVideo-1.5 and lowers BMR while restoring DD on Wan2.2-Turbo, with no significant difference in TCR, so constraint extent alone does not account for these artifacts. Together, these findings suggest that edited endpoints should serve as partial future goals, although edit relevance does not guarantee future validity. Details

Insight: 创新点在于从恢复操作和训练目标两个层面施加多级约束：log-LMS空间中的单调色变换限制颜色映射，误差校准的不确定性门控小波重建调制高频残差，参考锚定退化重放利用共享退化代理增强与输入的一致性；客观而言，其可借鉴之处是将退化一致性作为训练期约束且不引入推理开销。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement aims to correct color and recover detail degraded by wavelength-dependent attenuation, scattering, and noise. Paired-reference supervision constrains output similarity, but does not by itself specify the color mapping, the strength of local detail corrections, or consistency with the degraded observation. We propose MCL-UIE, a paired-supervised framework that addresses these requirements by constraining restoration operations and the training objective. Monotonic Chromatic Transform (MCT) restricts global color mapping in log-LMS space through positive diagonal entries and bounded cross-channel coefficients. Uncertainty-Gated Wavelet Reconstruction (UGWR) modulates high-frequency residuals with a learned confidence gate calibrated against normalized wavelet reconstruction errors. During training, Reference-Anchored Degradation Replay (RADR) renders the enhanced image and its paired reference using shared latent degradation proxies, encouraging both to reproduce the input. The three components share a restoration network that combines structured color correction, locally controlled detail recovery, and reference-anchored replay. MCL-UIE ranks first in thirteen of sixteen full-reference dataset-metric comparisons and produces competitive visual results on no-reference benchmarks. Objective ablations indicate that confidence calibration is most effective when combined with replay supervision. RADR is used only during training and adds no inference-time computation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wangtao-Bao/MCL-UIE.

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldFlow，一个联合预测未来视频和基于查询的 3D 轨迹的生成式世界模型。它将 3D 运动参数化为与视频网格对齐的连续位移 token，并用统一的 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干在 rectified-flow 目标下联合去噪视频与 3D 运动两种模态。作者在 144 万对象级样本上预训练，并提出 WorldFlowBench 进行评估。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型未显式建模 3D 动态，基于动作的方法受限于特定机器人构型，而 3D 运动预测模型多仅用 flow 监督，物理动态学习信号有限。论文希望联合视频与 3D 运动预测，显式耦合几何动态与视觉演化。

Result: 在 WorldFlowBench 的三个评估集上，WorldFlow 在所学方法中取得最低平均位移误差 ADE 和最终位移误差 FDE。在 SpatialCog 集上 ADE 为 0.060 m，相比最强外部基线降低 54.2%；相比仅 flow 变体，ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 55.9% 和 68.5%。

Insight: 创新点在于用与视频网格对齐的连续位移 token 表示 3D 运动，并通过统一 MoT 骨干联合去噪视频和 3D 轨迹，从而将显式几何动态与视觉演化耦合，释放预训练视频模型中的物理常识。大规模异构数据预训练和 WorldFlowBench 也提供了可借鉴的规模化验证方式。

Abstract: Predicting future physical states stands as a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing video world models leave 3D dynamics implicit, action-based formulations remain tied to specific robot configurations, and 3D motion prediction models use flow-only supervision, providing a limited learning signal for physical dynamics. To circumvent these limitations, we introduce \method{}, a generative world model that jointly predicts future video and query-based 3D trajectories. By parameterizing 3D motion as continuous displacement tokens aligned with the video grid, a unified Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone jointly denoises both modalities under a rectified-flow objective. This formulation couples explicit geometric dynamics with visual evolution, effectively unlocking the physical commonsense embedded within pretrained video models. To empirically scale and validate this unified architecture, we train \method{} on a heterogeneous mixture of 1.44 million object-level samples spanning simulated and real-world domains. We further introduce WorldFlowBench, comprising 1,678 evaluation samples from 11 data sources across simulated and real-world interactions, including spatial reasoning tasks. Across all three evaluation sets, WorldFlow achieves the lowest average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) among the evaluated learning-based methods. Notably, on the challenging SpatialCog set, WorldFlow achieves an ADE of 0.060 m, a 54.2% reduction compared with the strongest external baseline. It also reduces ADE and FDE by 55.9% and 68.5%, respectively, compared with its flow-only variant. Project page: https://worldflow-rbs.github.io/.

[94] Youtu-Parsing-Omni: One Encoder, One Schema, All Modalities cs.CVPDF

Yunhang Shen, Zhangquan Chen, Xiaotian Li, Zhongpeng Cai, Qing Zhou

TL;DR: 论文提出 Youtu-Parsing-Omni，一个全模态文档解析器，可将文档、图像、音频、视频统一转换为在时空上可定位的结构化记录，为智能体提供可读取、可定位、可引用证据的感知层。其核心设计是“一个编码器处理所有模态、一个 schema 统一所有任务”：Youtu-Omni-Encoder 由预训练语言模型初始化，以共享权重在统一时空坐标系中编码各模态，并通过少量融合层实现视频帧与同步音频的跨模态融合与时间对齐；OmniSchema 统一十种解析任务的输出，支持单一自演化后训练流程 OSAD。最终以单个 5B 参数模型在多个基准上取得领先结果。

Motivation: 现有文档/多模态解析系统通常按模态和任务各自设计编码器与输出格式，难以统一处理跨模态证据，也缺乏时空定位与可引用能力。论文希望用一个编码器和一个 schema 打通十种解析任务，把跨模态融合与时间对齐前移到感知阶段，为智能体提供统一的可定位证据来源。

Result: 单个 5B 参数检查点在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上取得 96.96 Overall 的 SOTA；在 OmniParsingBench 上以 75.08 Avg. 领先所有开放权重模型，优势集中在音频与视频；图表解析取得最佳综合分 95.02，化学指纹精确匹配度在对比系统中最高，排版乐谱的字符级转写与专用光学音乐识别模型相当，并显著优于通用系统。

Insight: 创新点在于：用语言模型初始化的共享权重编码器统一处理多模态，并在统一时空坐标系中显式建模视频—音频的融合与对齐；用统一 schema 将十种任务纳入同一 token 角色目标，配合基于自身 rollout、参考条件化监督并迭代刷新的在线策略蒸馏（OSAD），实现解析器与监督信号的共同演化。客观看，其“感知即融合”与“schema 统一 + 自演化后训练”的组合是较有借鉴价值的设计。

Abstract: We present Youtu-Parsing-Omni, an omni-modal parser that converts documents, images, audio, and video into structured records grounded in space and time, providing a perception layer for agents that read, locate, and cite evidence across modalities. Its design rests on one encoder for every modality and one schema for every task. Youtu-Omni-Encoder, initialized from a pre-trained language model to strengthen text reading, encodes all modalities with shared weights in a unified space-time coordinate system, and a few fusion layers let video frames attend to concurrent audio, bringing cross-modal fusion and temporal alignment into perception itself. OmniSchema unifies the outputs of all ten parsing tasks, allowing a single self-evolving post-training procedure, OmniSchema-Aware On-Policy Distillation (OSAD), to provide a shared token-role-based objective across tasks. In each round, the model is trained on its own rollouts against token-level targets from a reference-conditioned copy of itself, which is then refreshed with the trained weights, so that the parser and its supervision co-evolve. With a single 5 B-parameter checkpoint, Youtu-Parsing-Omni achieves state-of-the-art performance on OmniDocBench v1.6 (96.96 Overall) and leads all open-weight models on OmniParsingBench (75.08 Avg.), with its advantage concentrated on audio and video. It also achieves the best overall chart-parsing score (95.02) and the highest exact chemical-fingerprint agreement among compared systems, and matches a dedicated optical-music model in character-level transcription of typeset sheet music while staying far ahead of general-purpose systems. Code and model weights are publicly available.

[95] CAD-WAM: Anticipating Geometry for Text-to-CAD Generation cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Zhengyang Li, Weijie Zheng, Yuxin Cui, Sijie Wang, Wenjie Feng

TL;DR: 论文提出 CAD-WAM，用于从文本生成可编辑 CAD 模型。该方法将 CAD DSL 操作视为动作，利用预训练 VAE 将中间实体的表面点云编码为隐状态，并由 LLM 根据目标描述和当前几何状态生成下一步动作，同时用辅助头预测下一几何状态以提供监督。推理时通过 CAD 内核执行动作并重新编码实体，形成闭环多步生成。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的文本到 CAD 方法主要学习预测命令，缺乏对命令应产生何种几何状态的显式监督，单步错误会影响后续多步生成，导致可靠的多步 CAD 生成困难。

Result: 在 DeepCAD 测试集上，CAD-WAM 在几何精度和输出有效性上优于任务专用基线，在需要更多操作的模型上提升更明显，并优于使用相同 DSL 的通用 LLM；消融显示下一状态监督相比仅预测动作将平均 Chamfer 距离降低 13.2%。

Insight: 创新点在于将世界-动作模型思想引入文本到 CAD：用下一状态预测作为几何监督，并结合 CAD 内核执行与实体重编码形成闭环；从客观角度看，动作-状态联合建模和中间几何监督是提升多步 CAD 生成可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Generating editable computer-aided design (CAD) models from text requires selecting operations that produce the intended changes to the geometry constructed so far. An incorrect operation can compromise subsequent steps, making reliable multi-step generation challenging. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) primarily learn to predict commands, without explicit supervision of the geometric states those commands should produce. Inspired by world-action models, we introduce CAD-WAM, which couples geometry-conditioned action generation with next-state prediction. We define complete modeling operations as actions in a CAD domain-specific language (DSL) and encode surface point clouds of intermediate solids into compact latent states using a pretrained variational autoencoder. An LLM backbone generates the next action from the target description and current geometric state, while an auxiliary head on the same backbone predicts the next state to provide geometric supervision during training. We construct step-wise training trajectories from parametric CAD models in the DeepCAD dataset, pairing each trajectory with an expert-level textual description. At inference, each generated action is executed by a CAD kernel, and the resulting solid is re-encoded to inform the next decision. On the DeepCAD test set, CAD-WAM outperforms the task-specific baselines in geometric accuracy and output validity, with larger gains on models requiring more operations, and produces more accurate geometry than general-purpose LLMs prompted with the same DSL. Ablations further show that next-state supervision reduces mean Chamfer distance by 13.2% relative to an otherwise identical model trained only to predict actions. Code is available at https://github.com/Lzy-1037/CAD-WAM.

Details

Motivation: 现有视频融合方法主要依赖短程时间交互，长时信息利用不足，且融合表示不一致会导致闪烁伪影并破坏时间一致性。论文旨在同时解决长时时间信息未被充分探索和时间一致性不足这两个耦合问题。

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明 MemoryFusion 在融合质量和时间一致性上优于现有基于帧和基于视频的融合方法，但未给出具体 benchmark 名称或定量指标。

Abstract: Multimodal videos contain rich temporal cues that can facilitate faithful fusion. However, existing video fusion methods mainly rely on short-range temporal interactions, leaving valuable long-term information underexplored. Moreover, inconsistent fusion representations can cause flickering artifacts, undermining temporal coherence. To address these coupled challenges, we propose MemoryFusion, a two-stage framework integrating cross-temporal memory learning with progressive temporal refinement. Considering the complementary roles of adjacent and distant frames, the first stage combines a Short-Term Memory Bank for local temporal guidance with a Long-Term Memory Bank that preserves reliable historical information through a fusion-oriented update strategy. To mitigate temporal fluctuations, a Temporal Smoothing Module leverages soft motion cues for coarse background stabilization. Building upon the resulting fusion prior, The second stage further employs a lightweight 3D residual Temporal Refinement Module to correct remaining temporal inconsistencies while enhancing structural details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MemoryFusion achieves superior fusion quality and temporal consistency over existing frame-based and video-based fusion methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Meiqi-Gong/MemoryFusion.

Details

Motivation: 现有强化学习管线多训练单一角色智能体，仅依赖终端奖励完成搜索与回答，忽略了人类在搜索中检查证据、改写查询和修正结论的自我纠正过程，因此难以在完整交互轨迹上优化该过程。

Abstract: Multimodal tool-using agents augment vision-language models with access to external information and capabilities, enabling them to solve knowledge-intensive and visually complex tasks that cannot be addressed using visual context alone. However, existing reinforcement learning pipelines largely train single-role agents to search and answer using terminal rewards, overlooking how humans inspect retrieved evidence, reformulate queries, and revise conclusions during search. Consequently, they fail to optimize this self-corrective process over complete interaction trajectories. We propose MRC-MMAgent, a multi-role collaborative reinforcement learning framework that incorporates trajectory assessment and continuation directly into the executable agent loop. First, the framework establishes a multi-role collaborative loop that integrates solving, inspection, and continuation, enabling the agent to acquire evidence through tools, evaluate the preceding trajectory, and resume problem solving when the current answer is insufficiently supported. Second, we introduce a role-coupled learning objective that combines task success, interaction validity, and successful correction with full-trajectory policy optimization across the decisions generated by all roles. Extensive experiments show that, using only the original baseline training set without constructing additional datasets, our method consistently outperforms the corresponding base models and achieves leading or highly competitive performance across benchmarks.

Abstract: Visual commissions require image agents to integrate heterogeneous source materials, preserve requirements across dependent stages, and produce coordinated deliverables. TL;DR: 论文考察用文本人物设定（如“你有低视力”）提示视觉语言模型能否模拟所描述人群的感知。作者将persona回答与真实低视力、色盲或听障人群以及仅使用persona事实的简单规则比较，发现persona并不能带来相应视觉体验，反而抹平群体与个体差异。即使模型能估计模糊等对人的影响，文本描述也不会改变其图像信息或答案概率。

Details

Motivation: VLM正被越来越多地用persona提示来替代真实用户参与用户研究，论文质疑这种文本角色是否能让模型“看见”所描述者看到的内容。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly prompted with personas, such as “You have low vision”, to stand in for people in user studies. We ask whether a persona makes a model see like the person it describes. We compare persona answers with measured people with low vision, color blindness or hearing loss, and with simple rules that use only the facts the persona states. The persona never beats these rules, and it erases the real differences between groups and individuals. Describing blur leaves recognition unchanged in all 14 models we test, while blurring the photo lowers it as it does for people. Inside open models, the description changes neither the image information nor the answer probabilities. Yet the models know the effects they fail to show: they can estimate how blur and illusions affect people. Telling is not seeing: a persona makes a model say what it knows about people, not see what they see.

[101] Semantic Mismapping in Generalized Category Discovery: Evaluation and Mitigation cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Zitai Wang, Qianqian Xu, Cong Hua, Xi Chen, Zhiyong Yang

TL;DR: 论文研究广义类别发现（GCD）中部分可交换输出空间的评估偏差问题。作者发现，旧类索引已被监督语义锚定后仍使用全局匈牙利匹配，会把旧索引错误重分配给其他类别，从而隐藏预测错误，最高可使新类准确率高估25.63个点，称为语义错映射。为此提出保留语义的SPE评估协议，将匈牙利匹配限制在新类区域，并提出MMO优化，通过区域平衡损失和新类多样性损失缓解该问题，并从理论上给出虚假准确率增益上界。

Details

Motivation: 近期GCD方法使用可训练参数头，使旧类索引具有监督语义锚定、新类索引仍可交换，但评估仍采用全局匈牙利匹配，可能导致旧类语义被错误重映射并掩盖误差，因此需要揭示并修正这一评估与优化不一致问题。

Result: 摘要指出全局匹配可造成新类准确率最高25.63个点的高估；在GH和SPE两种评估下实验验证了所提方法有效，但未在摘要中报告具体benchmark数值或明确SOTA水平。

Insight: 创新点在于识别部分可交换输出空间中的语义错映射现象，给出置换环条件解释其如何提高全局匹配分数，并提出SPE评估协议与MMO优化相结合，同时从理论上分析虚假准确率增益上界，体现了评估协议与训练目标一致性的重要性。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to perform old-category recognition and novel-category discovery in the same unlabeled set. Early methods address both objectives through clustering and evaluate the exchangeable predicted indices with global Hungarian (GH) matching. Recent methods adopt trainable parametric heads, using supervision for old-category recognition and discovery-oriented objectives for new categories. In this way, the output space becomes partially exchangeable: old indices are semantically anchored, while new indices remain exchangeable. However, these methods still apply global matching, which can reassign anchored old indices to other categories and hide some prediction errors. Empirically, we are surprised to observe that this reassignment can induce an overestimation of new-category accuracy by up to 25.63 points. We term this phenomenon semantic mismapping and further derive a permutation-cycle condition that characterizes when semantic mismapping can increase the global matching score. To fix this issue, we propose Semantics-Preserving Evaluation (SPE), which preserves the supervised semantics of old indices and restricts Hungarian matching to the new region. Furthermore, we propose Mismapping-Mitigating Optimization (MMO) to alleviate the prediction errors that can give rise to semantic mismapping, combining a region-balance loss (RBL) for old/new routing with a novel-diversity loss (NDL) that encourages orthogonality among new prototypes. Theoretically, we show that minimizing the two losses can tighten an upper bound on spurious accuracy gains from semantic mismapping, with controllable residual terms. Empirical results under both GH and SPE demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

[102] Diagnosing the Reference Gap: What Visual Scaffolds Can and Cannot Fix in Multimodal Reasoning cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: 论文指出多模态大模型在视觉推理中的失败不一定源于感知，而是语言难以绑定到相关视觉实体、区域或位置，即“参考鸿沟”。作者构建ReGap-Bench配对基准，在五个视觉推理任务中固定场景、问题和答案，仅改变参考方式，发现任务匹配的外部参考（如编号）最多提升25%准确率，而不匹配参考收益很小甚至有害。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型能识别视觉内容却在多步推理中不可靠，例如计数时看见所有物体却丢失已计数的对象；论文试图说明这类问题常被误归因于感知，实际瓶颈是语言与视觉指代之间的绑定失败。

Result: 在ReGap-Bench的五个视觉推理任务上，任务匹配的参考可使准确率最多提升25%，而不匹配的参考几乎没有帮助，甚至降低性能。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将“参考鸿沟”作为独立于视觉感知的推理瓶颈，并通过配对基准控制场景、问题和答案来验证参考方式的作用；其可借鉴之处是构造任务适配的视觉参考可作为免训练的视觉技能提升多模态推理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can recognize visual content yet struggle to reason reliably over it across multiple steps. In counting, a model may see all objects but lose track of which ones it has counted. We argue that such failures, commonly attributed to perception, reveal a reference gap: the failure to bind language to the relevant visual entities, regions, or locations. Motivated by the theory of visual routines, we test whether external references such as numbering can narrow this gap. We introduce ReGap-Bench, a paired benchmark of five visual reasoning tasks in which the scene, question, and answer are fixed and only the reference varies. Results show that task-matched references improve accuracy by up to 25%, while mismatched references provide little benefit or even hurt performance. These findings identify the reference gap as a bottleneck distinct from visual perception and show that constructing task-appropriate visual references can serve as a training-free visual skill for improving multimodal reasoning.

Abstract: Formula-Driven Supervised Learning (FDSL) enables vision pre-training without real images, but existing methods cast pre-training as classification on discretized labels, discarding continuity in the generative process. We propose Formula-Driven Supervised Metric Learning (FDSML), which derives relational supervision from image-generation parameters beyond discrete class labels. FDSML combines FineFractalDB, our refined fractal dataset with higher intra-class variation and more stable structures, with a Parameter-Aware Triplet (PA-Triplet) Loss that assigns positives and negatives according to distances in the underlying Iterated Function System parameter space. Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) enable powerful multi-image synthesis but incur substantial computation from long visual contexts.
