Abstract: Compositional motion transfer requires a model to extract motions from multiple reference videos, and apply each motion to a user-specified object in one target scene. Existing methods normally struggle to reliably assign different reference motions to the correct target objects, thus a motion intended for one object may affect another. Meanwhile, some motion-specific tuning approaches are still facing another issue: they must run a new optimization whenever the reference video changes, making repeated transfers inefficient. To address these limitations, we propose RART, a framework that unifies object-wise motion extraction, target-region anchoring, and frame-aligned injection into a frozen video Diffusion Transformer for multi-object motion transfer. For each reference motion, RART extracts temporally ordered features from the masked reference video, and then conditions them on the target first-frame mask to form mask-anchored motion tokens. Frame-aligned motion cross-attention allows each generated frame to read only the motion tokens at the corresponding time slots, composing all object motions in one denoising process. During training, a motion-attention routing loss encourages each target region to attend to its corresponding motion tokens and reduces interference between objects. Therefore, RART can handle novel reference-target combinations without per-video optimization. Experiments show that RART achieves accurate motion preservation, efficient inference, and strong compositional generalization.

TL;DR: 论文提出 BEACON，用于连续手语分割的 begin/end 感知结构化解码器，结合四类 BIOE 标注、8 种合法转移约束的 Viterbi 解码，以及从二分类教师蒸馏边界知识到共享学生模型。论文在 PHOENIX-14 和 BSL Corpus 上验证其分割质量和下游识别效果，并分析不同分割指标与下游 WER 的相关性。

Motivation: 现有连续手语分割通常以局部边界检测为评价标准，但分割本质上是为下游手语识别服务，局部边界准确率未必能反映词法片段质量和识别收益。论文希望提升分割对下游识别的实用性，并重新审视更合适的分割评价指标。

Result: 在严格 no-cut 评估和基于验证集的模型选择下，BEACON 相对 SOTA 在 PHOENIX-14/BSL Corpus 上分别提升 segment-F1 14.58/14.59 点、boundary-F1 2.17/1.22 点，并降低下游 WER 9.10/4.15 点；相关性分析显示 segment-F1 比 boundary-F1 与更低 WER 更一致。

Insight: 创新点在于将 BIOE 四类标注、受约束的 Viterbi 结构化解码和边界知识蒸馏结合，用于连续手语分割；同时通过多架构实验指出应更关注 segment 质量和下游识别效用，而非仅看局部边界准确率。

Abstract: In segmentation-based continuous sign language recognition, an untrimmed video is first partitioned into lexical units; segmentation is therefore a means to an end—downstream recognition. However, segmenters are usually judged by local boundary detection rather than by the quality of the lexical segments and the recognition they enable. We present BEACON, a begin/end-aware segmenter that combines four-class Outside/Begin/Inside/End (BIOE) labeling, constrained Viterbi decoding over eight legal transitions, and boundary knowledge distilled from a binary teacher into a shared student. Under strict no-cut evaluation with validation-based model selection, BEACON improves segment-F1 by 14.58/14.59 points and boundary-F1 by 2.17/1.22 points, and reduces the downstream word error rate (WER) by 9.10/4.15 points relative to the state-of-the-art in the PHOENIX-14/BSL Corpus. To examine which segmentation metric better reflects downstream recognition, we evaluated five distinct architecture families. Segment-F1 aligns more consistently with lower WER than boundary-F1 in both corpora (Spearman $ρ=-1.0/-0.9$ versus $-0.9/-0.8$). These results support evaluating sign language segmentation in terms of segment quality and downstream recognition utility rather than local boundary accuracy alone.

Ziheng Xi, Weibo Li, Jiayi Yu, Sipeng Zheng, Wanpeng Zhang

TL;DR: TactoHand 提出从单目视频中预测手表面的三种密集交互场：接触、接近和3D力，从而避免大规模采集触觉数据。方法构建约5000万源帧数据，利用几何信息生成接触/接近标签、由运动生成力标签，并用时序Transformer聚合视觉证据、三个可学习查询解码对应场，同时以可微物理层约束力预测。

Details

Motivation: 研究出发点是人类手-物交互需要手表面的密集物理交互表示，但真实触觉传感数据依赖专用硬件、难以规模化采集。论文希望仅从单目视频低成本、可扩展地学习手-表面交互场。

Result: 在域内和域外benchmark上，TactoHand 对手面接触和接近估计优于先前方法；物理监督降低了运动rollout的位置和旋转误差；预测的接触与接近场还能提升下游手-物重建。

Insight: 创新点在于将几何导出的接触/接近标签与运动导出的力标签结合，用视频监督学习密集手-表面交互场，并引入可微物理层约束力预测。客观看，其可扩展、低成本的数据与学习路径以及面向下游重建的验证具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Understanding how humans interact with the world requires a representation of physical interactions at the hand surface. Tactile sensing captures these interactions, but collecting tactile data at scale requires specialized hardware. We introduce TactoHand, a model that predicts three dense hand-surface interaction fields—contact, proximity, and 3D force—from monocular video. We derive geometry-based contact and proximity labels and motion-derived force labels from eligible training data within a curated collection spanning approximately 50 million source frames. TactoHand uses a temporal transformer to aggregate visual evidence across frames, while three learned queries decode the corresponding hand-surface fields. A differentiable physics layer further constrains force predictions using observed object motion. Across in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, TactoHand improves contact and proximity estimation over prior methods. In addition, physics supervision reduces position and rotation errors in motion rollouts, and the predicted contact and proximity fields improve downstream hand-object reconstruction. Together, these results show a scalable, low-cost path to learning hand-surface interaction fields without collecting tactile measurements. Code will be released at https://github.com/zhenqis123/Tactohand.

Details

Motivation: 现有 VLM 常无法识别文化特定概念，尤其是欠代表地区，如何将这类长尾知识注入模型仍是挑战；论文探索知识编辑作为针对性替代方案。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently fail to recognize culture-specific concepts, especially from underrepresented regions. Effectively injecting this long-tail knowledge into existing models remains a challenge. In this work, we investigate knowledge editing as a targeted alternative by introducing CulturalBind, a benchmark comprising 3.2K images of 1K concepts from 6 domains, designed to evaluate culture-aware cross-modal knowledge as well as concept-level knowledge editing in VLMs. Pre-edit evaluation reveals that textual and visual knowledge is decoupled: models often demonstrate text-based knowledge of cultural concepts but fail to identify them from images. We then evaluate editing methods in single and sequential scenarios. Although current methods successfully correct individual visual recognition errors, they exhibit low cross-modal portability, failing to integrate edits into the model’s broader parametric knowledge. Furthermore, performance degrades rapidly under sequential editing, highlighting the need for comprehensive adaptation approaches that better consolidate multimodal knowledge over time.

[113] DiFuse: Fusing Native Diffusion Representations for Spatial Reasoning in Unified Multimodal Models cs.CVPDF

Ruixin Fan, Weining Wang, Jiahui Sun, Tailai Chen, Jing Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出 DiFuse，将统一多模态模型中原生扩散分支的中间表示选择性注入理解通路，以增强空间推理。方法通过逐层分析确定最有信息量的扩散表示及最佳注入位置，将扩散特征与视觉 token 空间对齐后，以残差融合方式注入多模态解码器层。实验表明其在不显著损害通用多模态理解的情况下提升空间推理能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型中，理解表示常用于条件生成，而生成/扩散表示很少被复用于多模态推理，信息流存在不对称。论文希望在不损害通用理解的前提下，利用扩散分支的中间表示补充空间推理所需线索。

Result: 在 UniWorld-V1 上，DiFuse 相比匹配的 SFT 基线持续提升空间推理，同时在通用多模态基准上保持大致相当的性能；消融分析表明增益关键依赖扩散表示选择、注入位置及扩散特征与视觉 token 的空间对应关系。OmniGen2 上的结果进一步证明其可迁移到其他统一多模态架构。

Insight: 创新点在于将 UMM 原生扩散分支的中间特征作为空间线索，并通过结构保持的选择性残差融合接入理解通路。客观看，其价值在于系统分析“用哪层扩散表示、注入到哪里、如何保持空间对齐”，为统一多模态模型中生成表示反哺推理提供了可迁移的融合范式。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly support visual understanding and image generation, yet information flow between these two capabilities remains largely asymmetric: understanding representations routinely condition generation, whereas generative representations are rarely reused for multimodal reasoning. In this work, we investigate whether intermediate representations from a UMM’s native diffusion branch can enhance spatial reasoning without compromising general multimodal understanding. We propose DiFuse, which selectively incorporates intermediate diffusion representations into the understanding pathway of UMMs. Through systematic layer-wise analysis, we identify which diffusion representations are most informative and where they can be integrated with minimal interference to semantic processing. Guided by these findings, DiFuse spatially aligns diffusion features with visual tokens and injects them into selected multimodal decoder layers via residual fusion. Extensive experiments on UniWorld-V1 show that, DiFuse consistently improves spatial reasoning over a matched SFT baseline while maintaining broadly comparable performance on general multimodal benchmarks. Further analyses show that these gains depend critically on the choice of diffusion representations, injection positions, and the preservation of spatial correspondence between diffusion features and visual tokens. Results on OmniGen2 further demonstrate the transferability of DiFuse across unified multimodal architectures. Overall, our findings show that native diffusion representations can provide complementary spatial cues for multimodal reasoning when selectively integrated in a structure-preserving manner.

[114] FutureWorld: A Plug-and-Play Causal Next-State Predictor for Interactive Video World Exploration cs.CVPDF

Guangyuan Li, Kelu Yao, Shuolin Xu, Xinlei Yu, Wei Xing

TL;DR: 论文面向交互式视频世界模型，指出现有基于历史潜变量复用的方法会因模糊细节、几何畸变、语义漂移及错误反复进入历史而导致误差循环累积。为此提出可插拔的因果下一块预测器 C-NCP，在生成下一交互视频块前，根据当前生成潜变量和下一动作预测未来场景状态，并用该未来状态先验引导后续生成，以提升长时程一致性和减少误差累积。

Details

Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型需要跟随用户动作并保持长时场景一致性，但历史复用会把已有生成误差带入后续片段并反复强化，形成误差再循环。论文希望在未来视频块生成前引入未来状态预测，减少从历史继承的错误。

Result: 作者将 C-NCP 集成到四个基础生成器中，并在三个交互式长视频生成与动作条件世界建模基准上评估。实验表明方法可减少误差累积并提升长时程一致性，但摘要未给出具体数值或明确 SOTA 结论。

Insight: 创新点在于将历史复用范式转为未来状态先验引导：用当前潜变量和下一动作预测下一块场景状态，并通过跨模型适配和预测引导调制实现即插即用。客观看，其价值在于以轻量模块打断误差再循环，并可适配多种生成器，强调长时交互中的因果预测与生成引导。

Abstract: Interactive video world models aim to follow user actions while maintaining scene consistency over extended interactions. History-based methods reuse generated latents to preserve scene content, but three limitations of this reuse contribute to error recirculation. 1) The latent generated at each interaction is retained as history, potentially containing blurred details, geometric distortions, or semantic drift. 2) Although retrieved historical content matches the current scene, it can still contain errors that are carried into the next chunk. 3) New outputs then become history for later interactions, potentially reinforcing the same errors through repeated reuse. To limit this cycle, we aim to predict the future scene state from the current generated latent and the next action before generating the next interaction’s video chunk. This future-state prior provides guidance for the next chunk, with the aim of reducing the errors carried forward from history. To this end, we propose a plug-and-play Causal Next-Chunk Predictor (C-NCP) with cross-model adaptability and prediction-guided modulation. We integrate C-NCP into four base generators and evaluate the augmented models across three benchmarks for interactive long-video generation and action-conditioned world modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate reduced error accumulation and improved long-horizon consistency across diverse generation settings. The project page is at https://GuangYuanKK.github.io/futureworld.

[115] Intrinsic Distribution Matching cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Deyuan Liu, Yansen Han, Kaiwen Zheng, Jun Xie, Jiacheng Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出内在分布匹配（IDM），用于单步/少步文本到图像生成。它在DMD分数差监督基础上，将反向KL场分解为高斯矩分量和非高斯残差，并显式匹配生成器EMA特征的一阶均值与逐坐标标准差，以提升少步生成对提示词细节的还原。方法无需外部特征编码器，在Z-Image-Turbo 6B和Qwen-Image 20B上验证了全参数与LoRA微调效果。

Details

Motivation: 少步/单步T2I在细化机会有限时仍需准确呈现提示词细节；DMD依赖分数差将生成分布拉近参考分布，论文探究显式矩约束能否改进这种学习到的分数监督。

Result: 在2次网络评估下，IDM在中文和英文LongText-Bench分别达到94.25%和90.20%，为该预算下对比方法最高；消融显示矩损失带来增益，且生成器EMA特征在五项指标上与外部DINOv2编码器表现相当。

Insight: 创新在于将反向KL场显式分解为高斯矩项与非高斯残差，在保留完整DMD校正的同时加入均值/标准差匹配；并利用生成器EMA副本提供特征统计，避免额外外部编码器，降低依赖且具竞争力。

Abstract: One-step and few-step text-to-image generation must accurately render the details specified in the prompt despite limited opportunities for refinement. Distribution matching distillation (DMD) uses score differences to bring the generated distribution closer to a reference distribution. Following prior work, we estimate this difference with internal velocity branches, avoiding a separate fake-score network. We ask whether an explicit moment constraint improves this learned score supervision. A decomposition of the reverse-KL field into a Gaussian moment component and a non-Gaussian residual identifies statistics that can be constrained explicitly. We introduce Intrinsic Distribution Matching (IDM), which retains the full DMD correction and matches feature means and per-coordinate standard deviations using the generator’s exponential-moving-average (EMA) copy, requiring no external feature encoder. Experiments use full-parameter and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) tuning on Z-Image-Turbo 6B and Qwen-Image 20B. At two network evaluations, IDM reaches 94.25% and 90.20% on Chinese and English LongText-Bench, the highest scores among the compared methods at this budget. Controlled ablations show gains from the added moment loss, while generator EMA features perform competitively with an external DINOv2 encoder across the five reported metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/IDM.

TL;DR: 论文系统刻画视觉语言模型在3D推理中的失败模式，提出受控基准BedBench，包含13个程序生成任务，覆盖单项判断与复合精确答案。作者评测12个开源权重模型和4个前沿模型，发现模型在逐颜色计数等精确属性恢复与一致使用上存在巨大差距，并主张从精确正确性、答案一致性和图像依赖性三方面评估3D推理。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型可能在某些单项判断上表现正确，却无法恢复并一致使用3D物体的精确属性；论文旨在刻画这一差距并定位失败发生在属性恢复还是属性使用阶段，而非仅看整体准确率。

Result: 在逐颜色计数任务上，人类准确率为93.3%，最佳模型仅7.2%；针对性微调可将某模型从3.0%提升至68.6%，但其对最常见颜色的回答仍与自身逐颜色计数不一致。探测Qwen3.5-27B显示逐颜色计数在语言模型输入处可恢复率达65.0%，但模型回答仅3.0%；推理、扩展和视觉提示能改善单项判断，却不能保证精确答案可靠，图像移除控制还表明部分表面成功不依赖视觉证据。

Insight: 创新点在于构建受控3D推理基准并区分单项判断与复合精确答案，揭示“能恢复属性”不等于“能一致使用属性”，并结合探针与图像移除控制分析失败来源。可借鉴之处是采用精确正确性、内部一致性和图像依赖性作为多维度评测标准，以更严格评估视觉语言模型的3D推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models can get individual judgments right while struggling to recover and consistently use the exact properties of a 3D object. To characterize this gap, we introduce BedBench, a controlled benchmark of thirteen procedurally generated tasks spanning individual judgments and composite answers. Across twelve open-weight and four frontier models, per-color counting exposes a pronounced weakness: humans achieve 93.3% accuracy, compared with 7.2% for the best model. Targeted fine-tuning raises one model’s accuracy from 3.0% to 68.6%, but reveals a further difficulty: its answers about the most common colors remain inconsistent with its own per-color counts. Supplying those counts as text improves its judgments, showing that recovering a property does not mean using it consistently. Reasoning, scaling, and added visual cues each improve individual judgments, but none makes exact answers reliable. To locate the difficulty, we probe Qwen3.5-27B: exact per-color counts remain recoverable at the language model’s input, yet the model’s answers rarely recover them (65.0% for the probe vs. 3.0% for the model). Image-removal controls further show that some apparent successes require no visual evidence. These findings motivate evaluating 3D reasoning by exact correctness, consistent use, and image dependence, rather than accuracy alone. Code and dataset are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/BedBench

Abstract: Colonoscopy is inherently comparative: reliable visual assessment of polyps often depends on complementary views acquired under different viewpoints and imaging conditions. [118] Look at Where You Think: Localization-Head Alignment for Fine-Grained Perception in Thinking VLMs cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文研究思维视觉语言模型在推理数千token后仍漏掉高分辨率图像细节的问题，发现瓶颈在于少数“定位头”对视觉证据的选择而非视觉感知本身。提出定位头对齐（LHA），在SFT和RL训练中将推理提到的视觉事实与定位头的注意力区域对齐，作为辅助损失。在V*、HR-Bench 4K和TreeBench上，LHA将Qwen3.5-4B准确率从74.1%提升到76.6%，为最佳单次推理4B结果，并缩短20%推理长度。

Insight: 创新点在于识别出少量定位头负责从大量视觉token中选择证据，并将注意力作为训练信号，与推理中陈述的视觉事实对齐。客观看，其创新是将可解释性发现转化为轻量辅助损失，无需额外推理pass，同时提升细粒度感知与推理效率。

Abstract: Thinking vision-language models reason for thousands of tokens, yet still miss small details in high-resolution images. Zooming methods pay for extra inference passes, and outcome rewards never tell the model where to look at. In Qwen3.5-4B, we find that the bottleneck is selection, not seeing: a few attention heads, which we call localization heads, pick the evidence out of thousands of visual tokens; ablating four of them costs 13.2 points more than ablating four random heads, and their focus fades as the reasoning grows. We therefore propose Localization-Head Alignment (LHA), which makes attention a training signal: whenever the reasoning states a visual fact, these heads learn to look at the focal region rather than the background, as an auxiliary loss in both SFT and RL training. On benchmarks of V*, HR-Bench 4K and TreeBench, LHA lifts Qwen3.5-4B from 74.1% to 76.6% accuracy, the best single-pass 4B result we evaluate, ahead of multi-pass zooming and released 7B models. Alignment alone adds 1.8 points over otherwise identical training while shortening reasoning by 20%, and the gains carry over to Qwen2.5-VL-3B.

[119] AB-Attention: Accurate Block Attention for Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers cs.CVPDF

Bin Xu, Mo Zhang, Zhengfeiyang Zhang, Xinfeng Yang, Yixing Ma

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的 Accurate Block Attention（AB-Attention），用于加速视频扩散 Transformer 推理。该方法通过块内聚类选择器、包含概率加权采样和细粒度核聚合，在高稀疏度下更准确地近似稠密 3D 注意力，从而显著减少注意力计算并实现端到端加速。

Details

Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 的 3D 全注意力二次成本主导推理开销；现有动态块稀疏注意力在激进稀疏下保真度下降，存在粗粒度评分、昂贵全局聚类、剪枝块从 softmax 消失以及整 tile 保留/丢弃过于粗糙等问题。论文旨在高稀疏条件下仍能准确近似稠密注意力，且无需训练。

Result: 在 Wan2.1 和 HunyuanVideo 上，AB-Attention 跳过 89%–92% 的注意力计算，而基线计算量约为其 1.8–3.6 倍；相比最强基线 PSNR 最高提升 4.4 dB，并实现最高 2.23 倍端到端加速。

Insight: 创新点包括：IBCS 在查询块内部聚类以低成本可靠估计块重要性且不重排 token；SIP 按包含概率采样块并用逆概率重加权，以单次估计稠密注意力而非直接截断；核实现将小于 tile 的 K/V 块聚合成完整 tensor-core tile，在不增加 tensor-core 成本下细化选择。客观上看，其价值在于免训练、高稀疏下降低估计偏差并提升选择粒度，实现较好的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers generate high-quality videos, but the quadratic cost of 3D full attention dominates their inference. Dynamic block-sparse attention reduces this cost by computing exact attention only on key/value blocks selected from current activations. However, its fidelity degrades under aggressive sparsity: blocks are scored from coarse summaries or formed by costly global clustering, pruned blocks vanish from the softmax, and whole tiles are kept or dropped even when only a few of their keys matter. To address these limitations, we propose Accurate Block Attention (AB-Attention), a training-free method that accurately approximates dense attention at high sparsity. Its Intra-Block Clustering Selector (IBCS) estimates block importance faithfully yet cheaply by clustering queries only within each query block, without permuting tokens. Sampling with Inclusion-Probability Weighting (SIP) then samples blocks in proportion to these estimates and reweights them by inverse inclusion probability, so that one pass estimates dense attention rather than truncating it. Finally, its kernel gathers key/value blocks much smaller than a tile into full tensor-core tiles, refining selection at no extra tensor-core cost. On Wan2.1 and HunyuanVideo, AB-Attention skips 89–92% of attention, whereas the baselines compute 1.8–3.6$\times$ as much, yet it still improves PSNR by up to 4.4~dB over the strongest baseline and achieves up to 2.23$\times$ end-to-end speedup. Our code is available at https://github.com/InfRec-Cardinal/AB-Attention.

Abstract: Agentic image editors decompose complex requests into steps, but when a step fails, they mostly retry with the same narrow set of tools and a judge that scores each output by eye directly. We argue that such agents are bounded by two spaces: the action space of what the agent can do, and the observation space of what its judge can see. On our expert-annotated benchmark, a general-purpose editor yields no usable result in over 40% of the cases, and no judge is reliable across edit types. We present EditAgent, which expands both spaces. It routes each step to general editors and specialized methods, and attaches to each candidate a verification record. With these records, off-the-shelf VLM judges outperform finetuned reward models. Candidates are generated in parallel and judged in one listwise call: experts prefer this to sequential refinement in 78.5% of decided comparisons, at no more than half the judge calls. Motivation: 现有迭代细化模型使用固定的更新计划，导致计算成本高且速度与精度难以兼顾，在视频流中尤为突出。论文旨在通过自适应收敛机制和时序状态转移，在保持高精度的同时显著降低计算延迟。

Abstract: Iterative refinement models have significantly improved stereo matching accuracy, but their rigid, fixed update schedules incur heavy computational costs and a strict trade-off between speed and accuracy. This challenge is magnified in video streams, where traditional online methods resort to reducing feature channels or relying on expensive temporal warping. To address this, we propose \textbf{DEQ-Stereo}, a novel video stereo matching framework based on the Deep Equilibrium (DEQ) paradigm. Instead of unrolling a fixed number of iterations, DEQ-Stereo reformulates the refinement as an implicit fixed-point problem, adaptively terminating once convergence is met. To ensure fast and stable intra-frame convergence, we introduce the \textbf{Convolutional Simplified Gated Unit (ConvSGU)}, which eliminates unnecessary non-linearities by modeling the latent state as a gated, element-wise sum of inputs. Furthermore, to exploit cross-frame temporal coherence, we propose \textbf{Temporal State Transfer (TST)}, a warm-start strategy that propagates the converged hidden state to the next frame without requiring explicit pose or warping priors. Details

Motivation: 视频步态评估中常保留高置信度姿态观测以降低测量误差，但这可能改变所评估的运动内容，需要区分测量误差与选择偏移带来的影响。

Abstract: Video-based gait assessment often keeps only high-confidence pose observations, which can lower measurement error but change the movement assessed. We compared confidence selection with budget-matched random selection in a Parkinson-related cohort (n = 96) and an NF2-related replication cohort (n = 18). In the Parkinson-related cohort, confidence selection produced a small but consistent shift in the observed movement summary (+0.05 SD, 95% CI 0.03 to 0.07), while held-out Mini-BESTest error stayed flat (-0.04 points, -0.13 to 0.05); a policy preserving motion-group proportions reduced the shift, while NF2 showed no shift and its predictor did not beat a constant baseline. Because this shift mixes measurement error with selection shift, we decompose and simulate both on the cohort’s window structure. Filtering lowers local error when error depends on confidence, but biases the endpoint once confidence tracks movement more than error. In the Parkinson-related data, confidence was only weakly tied to movement (rho = 0.14). There, filtering is harmless if well-tracked windows are unbiased. If instead systematic error only redistributes within participants, filtering can add up to 0.58 degrees. Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection aims to transfer anomaly discrimination from auxiliary training domains to unseen categories while localizing subtle defects. Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言模型的对齐目标可能奖励错误行为：均匀监督微调让简单样本主导更新，临床关键边界样本过早饱和；仅依赖最终答案正确性的稀疏奖励无法评估解剖 grounding 和临床逻辑，易导致答案捷径和 reward hacking。

Insight: 创新点在于将能力边界校准、反事实偏好学习和评分标准接地强化学习结合：用共享推理前缀但在关键诊断分支分叉的偏好对聚焦边界区域，并用预测熵自适应激活临床 rubric，同时抑制可靠样本上的 rubric 噪声。客观看，其可借鉴之处是把群体级边界先验与实例级不确定性耦合，以缓解医疗 VLM 对齐中的稀疏奖励和捷径学习问题。

Abstract: Medical diagnostic reasoning requires verifiable links between visual evidence and clinical knowledge, yet current medical vision language models are aligned with objectives that reward the wrong behavior. Uniform supervised fine tuning lets easy cases dominate updates while clinically critical boundary cases saturate prematurely. Sparse rewards based only on final-answer correctness cannot assess anatomical grounding or clinical logic, inviting reward hacking through answer shortcuts. We propose MedCBR, a two-stage alignment framework coupling boundary-aware preference optimization with rubric-grounded reinforcement learning. First, we construct counterfactual boundary preferences at the model’s capability frontier using empirical pass rates and preference pairs that share reasoning prefixes but diverge at decisive diagnostic branches. Difficulty Weighted Boundary Preference Optimization (DBPO) concentrates learning on this calibrated boundary region. Second, we distill reusable clinical rubrics from boundary trajectories and activate them according to prediction entropy. Entropy Gated Grounded Policy Optimization (EGPO) rewards answer correctness, anatomical grounding, and reasoning fidelity while suppressing rubric noise on reliable samples. A reference policy anchored to the boundary-aligned model couples population-level boundary priors with instance-level uncertainty, making diagnostic reasoning more accurate and clinically grounded. Experiments on six medical benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong general, specialized, and reinforcement learning baselines, reaching 61.2% average accuracy with the largest gains on challenging differential diagnosis tasks. Ablations show super-additive gains: combining the two stages exceeds the sum of their individual improvements.

Details

Motivation: 提示调优虽能参数高效适配视觉语言模型，但常导致基类欠自信、新类过自信的双重校准问题。现有校准方法缺乏对超球嵌入空间几何性质的理解，难以利用角度判别性调整扭曲的决策边界。

Result: 在15个数据集、多种骨干网络和9种提示调优方法上进行实验，M-PFT降低了新类平均校准误差，在基类上保持竞争力，并维持相近准确率；同时计算开销可忽略。

Insight: 创新点在于从超球嵌入空间的角度间隔出发，联合引入类间余弦相似度最小化和零样本语义锚定两个目标，以几何方式校准提示调优后的决策边界并保留预训练语义结构。

Abstract: Prompt-tuning has emerged as a parameter-efficient technique for adapting large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) to downstream tasks while keeping the pre-trained weights frozen. However, prompt-tuned VLMs often suffer from a base-to-novel dual miscalibration problem, where models typically show underconfidence on base classes and overconfidence on novel classes. Current VLM calibration approaches address this dual miscalibration either separately or jointly, but lack a geometric understanding of the hyperspherical embedding space and thus fail to exploit angular discriminative properties to adjust distorted decision boundaries. To address this, we propose M-PFT, a novel angular-margin approach for prompt tuning that introduces two complementary objectives to promote class discriminability and semantic anchoring. This enlarges the angular margin by 1) minimizing the maximum cosine similarity between tunable class embeddings to reduce embedding collapse and 2) minimizing the angular distance between each tunable embedding and its corresponding zero-shot embedding to preserve semantic geometry. Extensive experiments across 9 prompt-tuning methods with various backbones on 15 diverse datasets show that our approach reduces the average calibration error on novel classes, while remaining competitive on base classes and maintaining comparable accuracy. Further analyses reveal that M-PFT effectively adjusts distorted decision boundaries induced by prompt tuning while preserving the pre-trained embedding space, improving calibration with negligible computational overhead.

Abstract: Generating interactive 3D assets from a single image requires realistic appearance, fidelity to the depicted object, and plausible physical behavior. Abstract: Novel view synthesis (NVS) in degraded scenes aims to reconstruct 3D scenes from multi-view observations affected by various degradations, and to generate clear and photorealistic images at unseen viewpoints. To this end, we propose MLLM-GS, a multimodal large language model (MLLM) prior-guided framework for fast NVS in extreme environments. Abstract: Recent advances in gigapixel-level imaging have brought High-Resolution Wide shots to the forefront of research. This makes traditional close-up detectors inaccurate and slow as they are overwhelmed by the background. Although previous research has explored sparse backbones, their fixed sparsity patterns lack the adaptability required to handle diverse target numbers. To address this, we introduce ElasticFormer, a sparse backbone that dynamically allocates computational resources based on foreground proportion. After scoring windows based on variance, proposed ElasticSelector module will predict the foreground proportion for top-k selection. The mechanism guides the model to select target-containing windows, scaling resources in areas where objects are clustered. Abstract: Cone beam computed tomography (CBCT) is widely used for image guided and adaptive radiotherapy, but scatter, noise, reconstruction artifacts, and inaccurate Hounsfield units limit its quantitative use. Recent diffusion approaches for CBCT to CT synthesis remain largely image driven, with limited use of the underlying acquisition physics. We propose EPC-RawDC, a conditional 3D latent diffusion framework that combines volumetric generative modeling with two complementary constraints derived from the same scanner specific CBCT forward model. First, geometry equivariant projection constraint exploits the rotational equivariance of the CBCT forward operator by supervising the synthesized CT through correspondingly transformed reference projections. Second, raw data consistency uses noisy projection measurements of the individual acquisition as scan specific supervision during training. The framework was evaluated on two independent clinical cohorts with distinct scanner geometries and projection sources. For JUST, the physics constraints used measured clinical raw CBCT projections acquired with an Elekta system, whereas for SynthRAD2025 the projection data were simulated from the reference CT using a Varian geometry with Poisson photon statistics. EPC-RawDC achieved an SSIM of 0.97 in both cohorts and improved PSNR over DDPM by 2.66 dB on SynthRAD2025 and 2.10 dB on JUST, while reducing mean MAE by 20.3% and 19.0%, respectively. On SynthRAD2025, projection consistency and equivariant errors were reduced by 55.0% and 41.4% relative to DDPM. These results demonstrate that combining a volumetric diffusion prior with scanner geometry and scan specific projection measurements improves quantitative CBCT to CT synthesis across both simulated and measured projection domains. Code available after publication at https://github.com/ALZAHRAALTALIB/EPC-RawDC.

Motivation: 扩散模型中的概念定位已有较多研究，但新兴的 next-scale 视觉自回归模型内部概念路由仍未被探索；为实现生成模型安全、可控和可解释的适配，需要知道概念在何时何地涌现。

Result: 在 LoCoBench 上对 Infinity、HunyuanImage-3.0、InfinityStar 等模型探测表明定位位置可解释且与概念涌现因果相关；在概念擦除、个性化、对抗概念注入应用中，定向干预达到 SOTA，同时显著降低计算开销并保持良性效用。

Insight: 创新在于首个模型无关的视觉自回归概念定位框架，将 coarse-to-fine 的 next-scale 生成过程拆解为 Layer、Scale、Position 三维定位，并用 LoCoBench 避免上下文偏差、通过因果验证支撑应用；客观看，其价值在于把概念路由分析转化为可操作的安全/高效适配手段。

Abstract: Understanding the internal routing of visual concepts is crucial for the safe and controllable adaptation of generative models. While concept localization has been widely studied in diffusion models, the emerging paradigm of next-scale visual autoregressive models remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce LoCo, the first model-agnostic framework to localize where and when specific concepts emerge within autoregressive models. Driven by the native coarse-to-fine nature of next-scale generation, our method precisely maps conceptual knowledge across three distinct dimensions: Layer, Scale, and Position. To systematically localize and evaluate concept routing without context bias, we propose LoCoBench, a comprehensive dataset spanning 10 diverse categories. Extensive probing on image and video autoregressive models like Infinity, HunyuanImage-3.0, and InfinityStar shows that the localized positions are both interpretable and causally related to concept emergence. Building on these insights, we apply our localization method to three key applications: concept erasure, model personalization, and adversarial concept injection. Experiments demonstrate that our targeted intervention achieves state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing computational overhead while preserving benign utility. Overall, our findings offer insights into how conceptual knowledge is routed during autoregressive generation, introducing a practical pathway for more interpretable, efficient, and secure adaptation.

Abstract: Tumor delineation is required for radiotherapy planning, yet automatic segmentation is limited by errors, predominantly false positives, that distort dose and increase editing time. Abstract: Unified multimodal models can both generate and understand images within a single set of weights, offering an opportunity to use image synthesis as training supervision for visual comprehension. Details

Motivation: 无监督4D医学图像插值只能利用时间稀疏的端点观测，缺少中间帧监督；多个不同运动场可能具有相近端点一致性却产生不同中间解剖，形成运动歧义，且当需要从端点估计大量独立运动变量时难以约束。

Result: 在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上，该方法在无中间体积训练的方法中达到state-of-the-art，并与使用中间监督的方法相比具有竞争力。

Abstract: Unsupervised 4D medical image interpolation reconstructs intermediate anatomical states from temporally sparse observations without intermediate supervision. Distinct motion fields may attain comparable endpoint agreement while producing different intermediate anatomy, giving rise to motion ambiguity. This ambiguity is difficult to constrain when many independently adjustable motion variables must be estimated from the observed endpoints. We develop a framework that learns a motion manifold to restrict candidate motion fields while retaining sample-specific variation. Cross-Sample Spectral Basis Learning extracts recurring motion structure from endpoint-derived dense estimates and encodes it in a shared spectral basis. Sample-Conditioned Cayley Basis Reorientation uses predicted motion coordinates to rotate the effective motion directions for each endpoint pair without increasing the number of independent coordinates. Their composition defines a nonlinear family of complete velocity fields across endpoint pairs, accommodating anatomical and motion-state differences within a restricted representation. Integrating each predicted field supports bidirectional reconstruction at arbitrary intermediate times. Theoretical analysis establishes sufficient conditions under which endpoint observations distinguish nearby motions within the learned family. Experiments on ACDC and 4D-Lung demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods trained without intermediate volumes and competitive results against methods using intermediate supervision.

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将探针评测放到大规模检索场景中做系统压力测试，并通过替换负样本来源解耦“手写负样本难度”和“真实检索难度”，同时揭示基准相关性标签不完整对评测结论的影响。其可借鉴之处是提示VLM细粒度理解评测需要更完整的相关性标注和更接近真实检索的候选池，而不能仅依赖小规模匹配探针。

Abstract: Vision–language models (VLMs) are tested for fine-grained understanding with image–text matching probes, a model must prefer the correct image or caption over handcrafted negative. In retrieval, the correct item must beat thousands of alternatives. We measure how these two evaluation methods relate by using two probing benchmarks, \svo{} and SugarCrepe, for retrieval tasks. For 100 image and caption queries we judge the top-10 retrievals from full dataset of six VLMs, with human annotators and an LLM assessor. We than compare how well models rank pool of items on the same queries, while changing only where the negatives comes from: the benchmark, a random draw, or the judged-incorrect retrievals of other models. On \svo{} the accuracy of retrieving a positive over handcrafted negatives is significantly higher than for retrieved ones (the correct item is preferred in 85% against 77% of the time). On SugarCrepe the order is reversed: its handcrafted negatives are harder than the retrieved ones. Incomplete relevance labels explain most of the low retrieval scores on \svo{}, where the top-ranked item is correct for 54–90% of the queries under human labels against 6–21% under the benchmark’s labels. However, we detect no association between passing the probe for a query and retrieving a correct item first. [140] VideoMemBench: How Well Do MLLMs Remember in Video Conversations? cs.CVPDF

Zhengdong Hu, Chao Wang, Kaituo Feng, Huanjin Yao, Mingfei Lu

TL;DR: 论文提出 VideoMemBench，用于评测多模态大语言模型在单个长视频的多轮对话中维持并复用上下文信息的能力（称为 in-context video memory，即上下文视频记忆）。该基准为每个样本配对一个视频和一段长多轮对话，相邻轮次通过两类跨轮依赖相连：一类要求模型记住先前轮次中描述过的视觉片段，另一类要求模型记住自己先前的回答。论文构建了 326 个视频、2,476 轮对话的数据集，并对 20 多个开源和闭源多模态大模型进行了评测。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频 QA 基准中的问题相互独立、从不互相引用，且仅凭视频本身即可回答，因此无法衡量模型在多轮对话中跨轮检索和复用信息的能力，而这是长视频多轮交互的核心能力。

Result: VideoMemBench 包含 326 个视频、2,476 轮对话，其中 1,387 轮依赖先前轮次（含多父依赖和长程回调）。在 20 多个开源和闭源多模态大模型中，表现最好的 Claude Opus 5 总体得分也仅 45.7%，说明该能力仍极具挑战性。

Insight: 论文提出并形式化了“上下文视频记忆”这一概念，区分“情景依赖”（记住看到的）与“答案依赖”（记住说过的）；用必要性（没有前轮上下文则无法作答）和非泄漏性（问题不复述前轮信息）两条属性约束数据构建，并设计 planner、generator、judger 三角色 LLM 流水线加人工专家审核来保证基准质量，可借鉴于其他多轮记忆评测的设计。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can now process hour-long videos, making extended multi-turn dialogue over a single video a natural mode of use. Sustaining such a dialogue requires more than answering each question in isolation: the model must retrieve and reuse information established earlier in the conversation, both what it has seen and what it has said, an ability we term in-context video memory. Existing video QA benchmarks leave this ability unmeasured: their questions are mutually independent, never reference one another, and each is answerable from the video alone, so nothing ever requires carrying information across turns. We introduce VideoMemBench, in which each sample pairs a single video with a long multi-turn dialogue, whose turns are connected by typed cross-turn dependencies: an Episodic Dependency refers back to a visual episode described in an earlier turn, probing memory of what the model has seen, while an Answer Dependency builds on the model’s own earlier answer, probing memory of what the model has said. Every dependency is verified considering two properties: 1) necessity, meaning the question cannot be answered without its earlier turns, and 2) non-leakage, meaning the question does not restate the information from its earlier turns. To enforce these properties, we design a construction pipeline with three LLM roles: a planner, a generator, and a judger, with each dialogue reviewed by human experts. VideoMemBench comprises 326 videos with 2,476 turns, of which 1,387 depend on earlier turns, including multi-parent dependencies and long-range call-back. Among over 20 evaluated open- and closed-source MLLMs, Claude Opus 5 performs the best with an overall score of only 45.7%, highlighting the challenge of in-context video memory. Our dataset and evaluation are released.

Abstract: Given a set of procedural steps, Video Step Grounding (VSG) aims to identify which steps occur in a video and localize when they are performed. Existing methods are either offline, requiring access to the full video before making predictions, or online, but relying on fixed-length video segments producing noisy, over-segmented outputs. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Step Decomposition (HSD), a training-free framework for online VSG that leverages the semantic understanding capabilities of Large Multimodal Models. HSD addresses two central challenges in online grounding: constructing semantically coherent temporal segments from a streaming video, and bridging the semantic gap between coarse procedural step descriptions and visual observations. To construct elementary temporal units, HSD incrementally groups incoming segments into semantically consistent events, using an anchor caption that is continuously validated against the visual stream. Based on these events, we perform zero-shot procedural step classification and selectively refine uncertain predictions by decomposing candidate steps into finegrained substeps that capture more nuanced visual evidence. By operating on semantically coherent events rather than every fixed-length segment, HSD reduces redundant computation and produces more stable predictions. Experiments on HT-Step, Ego4D Goal-Step, and CrossTask show that HSD substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods, overall achieving better step recognition, more accurate temporal localization, and faster inference.

Abstract: Event cameras capture visual brightness changes under challenging lighting conditions. TL;DR: 论文提出 AdMuse，一个技能引导的多智能体广告图像生成框架。它先将高质量广告抽象为可复用的创意技能库，用于跨域广告策略；生成时通过技能路由、多候选构思与选择、VLM 引导的视觉精炼，把广告创意从单次提示写作转化为结构化决策过程，从而生成既吸引注意又能传达产品定位的广告图像。

Details

Motivation: 创意广告图像需要兼顾吸引力和产品定位传达，而在渲染前选择有说服力的概念桥梁十分困难。现有方式多依赖检索孤立样例，难以跨域复用广告策略。

Result: 实验覆盖多样产品和品牌定位，显示 AdMuse 能生成视觉上更有吸引力且忠实传达产品定位的广告；摘要未提及具体定量指标、benchmark 或 SOTA 对比。

Insight: 创新点在于将高质量广告中的重复桥接模式抽象为可复用技能库，并结合技能路由、多候选构思选择与 VLM 反馈精炼，把广告创意建模为多智能体结构化决策流程，而非单步提示生成。

Abstract: An outstanding creative advertising image should both attract attention and communicate a product’s positioning. How to choose a persuasive conceptual bridge before rendering begins is challenging for advertising generation. We present AdMuse, a skill-guided multi-agent framework that distills high-quality advertisements into reusable creative skills for cross-domain advertising strategy. Rather than retrieving isolated exemplars, AdMuse abstracts recurring bridge patterns into a skill library that can guide new product briefs. At generation time, AdMuse coordinates skill routing, multi-candidate ideation and selection, and VLM-guided visual refinement: diverse skills propose alternative concepts, relative judgment selects the most promising direction, and vision-language feedback helps realize the selected intent in the final image. This design treats advertising creativity as a structured decision process rather than a single prompt-writing step. Experiments across diverse product and brand positionings show that AdMuse produces visually compelling advertisements that faithfully communicate product positioning.

Details

Motivation: 视频生成正从端到端合成转向依赖规划、工具使用和反馈的智能体化生成，同时视频世界模型可作为智能体交互环境；论文旨在通过可执行运行时框架厘清二者结合的系统设计与评估问题。

Result: 摘要未报告具体定量实验或benchmark结果；作为报告/综述性工作，主要给出三方向的分析框架、能力清单与开放挑战。

Abstract: Video generation is shifting from end-to-end synthesis toward agentic generation, where planning, tool use, and feedback enable coherent, controllable multi-shot videos. In parallel, video world models provide action-conditioned observations and predicted futures, serving as interactive environments for agents. We study their intersection through the agent harness: an executable runtime that constructs context, exposes tools, maintains state, routes feedback, and manages budgets around video models. This report examines three directions. First, agentic video generation, where harnesses coordinate scripting, shot planning, asset management, production, and editing, alongside the evaluation challenges of these systems. Second, agentic world modeling, where harnesses mediate observation, control, execution, and state. We discuss hybrid designs in which game engines or agent-written programs maintain world state while video models render observations, and how agents and world models can mutually evaluate and improve one another. Third, recursive self-improvement (RSI) in video model development, distinguishing output revisions, updates to harness state, generator parameters, and evaluators, and changes to the improvement procedure itself. We identify how these improvements feed back into subsequent development. Across these directions, we analyze essential agent capabilities, including audiovisual perception, long-horizon planning, memory, code-based interaction, feedback-guided control, skill reuse, and multi-agent coordination. We conclude with challenges in autonomous video production, lifelong memory, programmable hybrid control, autonomous model development, provenance, cost accounting, and evaluation linking video quality and task performance to sustained improvements in models and harnesses.

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个基于CIE色度学框架的五任务基准，用于评测多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的颜色判断能力，任务包括自动白平衡配对判断、肤色偏置方向、相关色温估计、CIE Delta E 2000色差配对排序和变化光照下的颜色命名。作者测试了八个MLLM端点，发现模型能处理粗略的二值判断，但在数值色温估计和精细色差判断上仍较弱。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型已被用于零样本图像质量判断，但其颜色判断很少用色度学真值进行检验。论文旨在建立可计算CIE真值的基准，系统评估MLLM颜色判断是否可靠。

Result: 模型通常能完成粗粒度的二值AWB判断，但在数值CCT估计和部分精细色差判断上表现较弱。在ColorChecker-24刺激上，三个闭源前沿模型的CCT平均绝对误差为715-1056 K，五个开源权重模型为1358-2133 K；非ColorChecker对照表明该优势部分依赖模板。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于提出覆盖多类颜色判断任务的CIE可计算真值基准，并揭示MLLM具备颜色词和粗视觉先验、但精细色差校准不可靠的问题。客观来看，其价值在于将多模态模型颜色能力评测与色度学指标及模板偏差控制结合，提供了更可量化的诊断方式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are now used as zero-shot judges of image quality, but their color judgments are rarely tested with colorimetric ground truth. We present a five-task benchmark based on the CIE colorimetric framework: AWB pair judgment, skin-tone bias direction, CCT estimation, CIE Delta E 2000 pair ordering, and color naming under changing illuminants. We test eight MLLM endpoints, including four Qwen3-VL variants, GLM-4.5V, GPT-5.4, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3.1-Pro. The models often solve coarse binary AWB judgments, but they remain weak on numeric CCT estimation and on some fine color-difference judgments. On ColorChecker-24 stimuli, the three closed-weight frontier models reach CCT MAE of 715-1056 K, compared with 1358-2133 K for the five open-weight models. A non-ColorChecker control shows that this advantage is partly template-dependent. The results suggest that current MLLMs have useful color words and coarse visual priors, but their color judgments are not reliably calibrated to CIE-computable color differences.

Guangzhi Wang, Junfu Pu, Yiran Wang, Xingyilang Yin, Honghao Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 TaskFrame，一个连接任务级规划与帧级键鼠控制的层次化规划器-控制器框架，用于构建通用游戏智能体。视觉语言任务规划器将高层目标分解为原子指令序列，控制器基于目标嵌入和近期观测在每个游戏帧输出离散键鼠动作与连续相机运动。系统采用规划器训练、控制器预训练和联合对齐三阶段训练。

Details

Motivation: 通用游戏智能体既需要高层任务推理，又需要在快速变化的视觉环境中进行低延迟动作控制；这两类能力对应的模型计算特性差异很大，难以用单一模型同时实现。

Result: 在 Minecraft 实验中，TaskFrame 的指令分解能力与通用视觉语言模型相当；在闭环评估中，智能体的战斗成功率高于所评估的基线方法。

Insight: 创新点在于层次化规划器-控制器架构：用视觉语言规划器生成原子指令，并将终止指令的特殊目标 token 隐状态作为可缓存复用的连续目标嵌入；因果 Transformer 控制器结合目标嵌入与近期观测预测离散键鼠动作和连续相机运动，并通过三阶段训练实现规划与控制对齐。

Abstract: Building general game agents requires both high-level task reasoning and low-latency action control in rapidly changing visual environments. These capabilities favor models with substantially different computational profiles, making them difficult to realize within a single model. In this work, we present TaskFrame, a hierarchical planner-controller framework that connects task-level planning with frame-level keyboard-and-mouse control. A vision-language task planner decomposes a high-level goal into an ordered sequence of atomic instructions and is optimized through supervised learning and dependency-aware GRPO. The hidden states of special goal tokens that terminate each atomic instruction provide continuous goal embeddings that are cached and reused across control steps. A causal Transformer controller combines the embedding of the active goal with recent observations to predict discrete keyboard and mouse-button actions and continuous camera motion at every game step. We train the system in three stages: planner training, controller pretraining, and joint alignment. Experiments in Minecraft show that TaskFrame is competitive with general-purpose vision-language models on instruction decomposition, and that in closed-loop evaluation the agent achieves a higher combat success rate than the evaluated baselines.

[147] LoRe-MIM: Lost-or-Replaced Masked Image Modeling for Self-Supervised Representation Learning in Whole-Body PET/CT cs.CVPDF

Amartya Bhattacharya, Bashirul Azam Biswas, Biratal Raj Wagle, Michael S. Leapman, Matthew E. Maeder

TL;DR: 论文针对全身PET/CT标注稀缺问题，构建了包含6,531例多中心、多示踪剂全身PET/CT扫描的数据集，并提出LoRe-MIM自监督框架，通过掩码图像重建学习多模态表示。其核心是让掩码区域要么置零（Lost），要么替换为对应互补模态（Replaced），以同时捕获模态特有信息和跨模态关系。

Details

Motivation: 全身PET/CT深度学习模型依赖专家标注，时间和成本高；自监督学习虽可缓解标注稀缺，但在全身PET/CT上因大规模数据有限和跨模态互补表示学习困难而研究不足。

Result: 在多模态PET/CT图像重建、patch级癌症/非癌症分类、全身病灶分割、标签高效训练和鲁棒性评估等下游任务上，LoRe-MIM均一致优于相应的最先进基线。

Insight: 创新点在于提出Lost-or-Replaced掩码策略，将掩码区域置零或替换为互补模态图像，以自监督方式学习模态特异特征与跨模态关系；客观上其价值还体现在构建多中心、多示踪剂全身PET/CT数据集并系统验证表示学习效果。

Abstract: Deep learning models for whole-body positron emission tomography/computed tomography (PET/CT) hold significant clinical potential for disease detection, treatment planning, and response assessment. However, the time and cost associated with expert annotations remain major bottlenecks in developing robust and generalizable models. Although self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising approach to mitigating annotation scarcity, its application to whole-body PET/CT remains relatively underexplored due to the limited availability of large-scale datasets and the challenges associated with learning complementary cross-modal representations. To address these limitations, we curate a heterogeneous, multi-tracer, multi-institutional cohort comprising 6,531 whole-body PET/CT scans and propose LoRe-MIM (Lost-Or-Replaced Masked Image Modelling), a novel self-supervised framework designed to learn multimodal representations through masked image reconstruction. Specifically, LoRe-MIM introduces a masking strategy in which image regions are either replaced with zeros (Lost) or substituted with corresponding complementary imaging modality (Replaced), encouraging the model to capture both modality-specific characteristics and cross-modal relationships. We evaluate the learned representations across multiple downstream tasks, including multimodal PET/CT image reconstruction, patch-based classification into cancer vs. non-cancer, whole-body lesion segmentation, label-efficient training, and robustness assessment. Experimental results demonstrate that LoRe-MIM consistently outperforms the corresponding state-of-the-art baselines across these tasks, highlighting its potential as a scalable and generalizable self-supervised learning framework for whole-body PET/CT analysis.

[148] Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Yubo Cui, Xianchao Guan, Zheng Zhang

TL;DR: 论文关注视觉语言模型（VLMs）在零样本场景下的对抗鲁棒性问题。作者分析发现，现有对抗微调中的预训练引导方式存在互补缺陷：匹配视觉特征覆盖广但缺乏语义重点，匹配预测分布有语义重点但受候选文本和预测概率限制。为此提出文本响应感知引导（TRAG），利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应来约束视觉表示漂移，并通过图像-锚点相关性聚合锚级约束，形成图像自适应约束。实验表明该方法在多种架构和任务上取得SOTA的干净与鲁棒性能，并有助于保持预训练图文关系。

Details

Motivation: VLMs零样本泛化能力强但对对抗攻击脆弱；现有对抗微调虽用预训练模型引导保持图文对齐，但视觉特征匹配和预测分布匹配各有局限，可能损害预训练图文关系并降低零样本性能。

Result: 摘要在多种架构和任务上的广泛实验显示，TRAG取得SOTA的干净性能和鲁棒性能；进一步分析表明其有助于保持预训练的图像-文本关系。摘要未给出具体数值或具体benchmark分数。

Insight: 创新点在于用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应方向和幅度构造语义锚点级约束，并借助预训练图像-锚点相关性聚合为图像自适应约束，从而在保留语义重点的同时约束视觉表示漂移。客观看，该方法将文本编码器的响应信号引入对抗微调引导，兼顾全局特征约束的覆盖性与预测分布约束的语义性，并规避候选文本和预测概率的限制。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image–text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features provides broad coverage by constraining all directions equally, but lacks semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image–text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. To address these issues, we propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder’s responses to local variations in input embeddings. Specifically, TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image–anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image–text relationships.

Yuanbo Lyu, Changjian Pan, Saining Zhang, Yi Yang, Peishuo Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 RFW-A，将智能体程序合成与程序化生成结合，用于大规模生成铰接资产。方法从已有铰接程序出发，把可复用组件及依赖组织为类别级生成器，由智能体采样配置和运动状态进行测试与精炼；生成器验证后可通过程序执行批量产出资产，无需继续调用模型。作者构建了覆盖 500 多类、超过 40 万个仿真就绪铰接资产的 RFW-A-400K，并验证其结构/运动学有效性及下游训练和零样本迁移效果。

Details

Motivation: 高质量铰接资产对具身感知、仿真和操作很重要，但人工标注和程序建模成本高；现有智能体合成方法虽能自动构建资产，却需为每个新资产重复昂贵的推理与精炼。论文观察到同一类别资产可共享构造逻辑，因此希望将智能体合成与程序化生成结合，以降低规模化生成成本。

Result: RFW-A 构建的 RFW-A-400K 包含 40 万以上、500 多类仿真就绪铰接资产，实验显示较高结构和运动学有效性，并能在物理模拟器中稳定执行。成本上，生成器编写平均约 10.62 美元，按 200 个资产摊销约 0.05 美元/资产，低于逐资产智能体合成基线的 1.24 美元/资产；用该数据训练可提升可见和未见类别的部件级铰接估计，仿真策略结合检索和程序编辑资产可零样本迁移到真实铰接物体。

Insight: 核心创新是把类别内共享的构造逻辑抽象为可复用、可测试和可精炼的类别级生成器，使智能体只需构建并验证一次程序，后续通过程序化执行摊销模型调用成本。客观看，其价值在于将智能体程序合成从逐资产生成转为类别级程序复用，并以大规模数据集和下游任务验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: High-quality articulated assets are essential for embodied perception, simulation, and manipulation, yet constructing them at scale remains difficult. Manual annotation and procedural modeling require substantial human effort. Recent agentic systems automate asset construction through program synthesis, but repeat costly reasoning and refinement for every new asset. Our key observation is that assets within a category can share construction logic despite variations in structure and appearance. Agentic synthesis and procedural generation are therefore complementary: agents can construct reusable programs, while procedural execution reuses the resulting logic across many samples. We therefore present RFW-A, which combines the two in a new articulated-asset generation paradigm. Starting from existing articulated programs, RFW-A organizes reusable components and their dependencies into category-level generators. Agents test and refine these generators across sampled configurations and motion states. Once constructed and validated, the generators produce diverse assets through procedural execution with no further model calls or token charges. The estimated API cost of generator authoring averages $10.62, amortizing to $0.05 per asset over 200 assets, versus $1.24 for the sampled per-asset agentic synthesis baseline. Using RFW-A, we built RFW-A-400K, containing over 400K sim-ready articulated assets across more than 500 categories. Experiments show high structural and kinematic validity and stable execution across physics simulators. Beyond asset validity, training with RFW-A-400K improves part-level articulation estimation on seen and unseen categories, while policies trained in simulation with retrieved and program-edited assets transfer zero-shot to real articulated objects.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation. To address these limitations, we propose HiFOR, a hierarchical foresight framework with action-guided world retrieval. Specifically, Hierarchical Foresight Prediction jointly predicts short-term state evolution, semantic milestones, and final goal states through a Hierarchical Dependency Mask (HDM), establishing dependencies across temporal horizons. Abstract: Images reveal appearances, but counting often asks about physical objects. A single entity may be visible directly, in several reflections, or through glass, so detecting every visible occurrence does not determine how many entities are present. Existing visual counting benchmarks rarely separate these two operations. We introduce PhysCount, a benchmark for evaluating whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can distinguish what appears in an image from what physically exists in the scene. PhysCount contains 546 real-world images and 4,595 human-written questions organized into 651 target-specific groups. For each group, annotators locate the relevant appearances, label how each is observed, and identify appearances of the same entity. Four complementary question families test the enumeration of appearances, the enumeration of physical entities, image-formation-specific counting, and appearance–entity correspondence. We measure both accuracy on each family and the proportion of groups answered entirely correctly. Across 40 MLLMs, counting visible appearances is substantially easier than recovering the identities that connect them. Higher image resolution improves access to visual evidence but brings limited gains in complete-group accuracy, whereas answering related questions jointly produces a marked improvement. These results expose a gap between recognizing what is visible and correctly recovering the physical entities behind those observations.

Result: 摘要未给出具体数据集和数值，但宣称 RADIOlogy 无需密集窗口执行，并在训练无关基线中显著优于其他方法；同时可通过少量修改成功应用于医学领域的零样本异常检测。

Abstract: Current zero-shot anomaly detection methods often rely on complex prompt-learning techniques or computationally expensive sliding-window strategies to achieve fine-grained visual alignment to detect small-scale anomalies. This reliance stems from the fundamental limitation of standard CLIP architectures, where [CLS] token attention and spatial patch pooling struggle to capture subtle, localized anomaly features. While prompt-learning approaches aim to bridge this gap, they typically require a large number of ground-truth segmentation masks from auxiliary datasets, which are often unavailable in specialized domains. In this work, we show an emerging property of multi-head attention pooling in vision-language models: remarkable zero-shot anomaly localization and detection right out of the box. We translate this property into a single-pass anomaly detection technique, RADIOlogy, that does not require dense, windowed execution and significantly outperforms other training-free baselines. Instead, the method relies on a simple contrastive scoring approach that compares the average similarity between vision features and a suite of normal and abnormal prompts. Furthermore, we leverage the agglomerative features of RADIO models to enhance pixel-level anomaly segmentation using a dense anomaly refinement pipeline. Finally, we show how the vision-language family of training-free zero-shot anomaly detection techniques can be applied successfully to the medical domain with minimal modifications. Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽能生成看似合理的战斗画面，但难以保证受击反应、血量更新等与游戏规则一致，缺乏持久状态和可扩展玩法机制。

Result: 实验表明FWM在动作保真度、受击反应、血量状态一致性和角色外观保持方面优于现有游戏世界模型；训练数据涵盖52种技能效果、187种角色外观变体和15个竞技场，且无需重训练渲染器即可支持多种修改规则。

Abstract: A playable, programmable game world must maintain persistent state, enforce executable rules, and support extensible gameplay mechanics. Existing video world models can generate plausible combat, yet struggle to keep hit reactions and health updates consistent with game rules. We introduce Fight World Model (FWM), which separates rule-governed combat execution from video rendering. An executable engine maintains state and resolves interactions. A deterministic compiler encodes these outcomes and attempted actions in a spatially and temporally aligned interaction proxy. The proxy aligns action and skill windows, brief hit reactions, and persistent health by fighter identity and timing. These cues can be controlled separately to render interactions under modified rules. Identity-bound references preserve character appearance across side switches. We train FWM on FightWorld Corpus, spanning 52 skill effects, 187 character-appearance variants, and 15 arenas. Details

Motivation: 交互式世界模型需在长时程探索中保持空间一致性并实时生成，但现有方法依赖时序上下文或显式3D记忆，内存随生成流长度增长；显式3D记忆还需重编码RGB渲染，难以实时。

Result: 实验表明L3G-World达到SOTA生成质量和长程空间一致性；相比最高效的先验3D记忆基线，3D基元最多少38.6倍、记忆更新快3.2倍、记忆渲染快359倍，并实现9 FPS实时世界生成。

Abstract: Interactive world models generate visual content conditioned on user actions, yet maintaining spatial consistency over long-horizon exploration in real time remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on either temporal context or explicit 3D memory, both of which scale poorly because memory grows with the length of the generated stream. Moreover, explicit 3D memories condition latent video generators by re-encoding RGB renderings, which precludes real-time generation. In this paper, we introduce a Latent 3D Gaussian Memory (L3G) that combines the efficiency of feed-forward reconstruction with a compact, rapidly renderable scene representation. Building on this memory, we propose L3G-World, in which a memory updater and a video diffusion transformer (DiT) alternate autoregressively to generate spatially consistent worlds. The memory updater spawns Gaussians in newly observed regions and refines existing ones, so that the memory grows with scene extent rather than stream length. Each Gaussian further stores a VAE latent feature, allowing the memory to be rendered directly into the DiT’s latent space without VAE re-encoding. Experiments show that L3G-World achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and long-horizon spatial consistency, with up to $38.6\times$ fewer 3D primitives, $3.2\times$ faster memory updates, and $359\times$ faster memory rendering relative to the most efficient prior 3D-memory baseline for each metric, enabling real-time world generation at 9 FPS.

[164] Read, Allocate, Route: Role-Coupled Tokenization for Pixel-Space Generative Transformers cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LG | cs.NEPDF

Jiongfan Yang, Weichen Fan, Haiwen Diao, Zhongang Cai, Ziwei Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出 SpecSlot，一种像素空间 rectified-flow Transformer，通过将每个空间锚点扩展为多个 DCT 频带 slot，把输出细节与全局注意力序列长度解耦。仅最低频 slot 参与全局注意力，所有 slot 进行局部交互，从而在不扩大全局序列的情况下提升频谱细节。方法在 ImageNet-256/512 和文本到图像生成上验证了更高效率与竞争力。

Details

Motivation: 像素空间生成 Transformer 通常每个图像 patch 一个 token，提升细节需要更多 token，导致全局注意力成本快速增加。作者观察到低频结构需要全局通信，而高频细节可局部建模，因此希望解耦细节建模与全局序列长度。

Result: ImageNet-256 上最终 FID 与 FLOPs 匹配的 JiT 控制组相当且收敛更快；ImageNet-512 上 FID 提升 2.84、FLOPs 少 3.9%，并以低 38.2% 成本接近 JiT-B/16（FID 差 0.69）；文本到图像 DPG-Bench 上以低 40.6% 前向成本超过 JiT-B/8 1.06。

Insight: 创新点在于角色耦合的 tokenization：按 DCT 频带拆分 slot，让低频 slot 负责全局注意力、高频 slot 局部交互，并用空间头做局部校正。客观看，其价值在于将频谱细分与锚点密度分离，使细节提升不再线性增加全局注意力开销。

Abstract: Pixel-space generative Transformers typically use one token per image patch, so capturing finer detail requires more tokens and makes global attention increasingly expensive. We observe that finer detail does not necessarily require more global communication: low-frequency structure benefits from global exchange, while high-frequency detail can be modeled locally. Based on this insight, we introduce SpecSlot, a pixel-space rectified-flow Transformer that decouples output detail from the length of the global attention sequence. SpecSlot expands each spatial anchor into multiple slots, each assigned a disjoint band of DCT coefficients; this separates anchor density from spectral subdivision. Only the lowest-frequency slot from each anchor participates in global attention, while all slots interact locally. This allows the model to increase spectral detail without enlarging the global sequence. A spatial head further adds a local correction to the spectral prediction, while the generative state, target, and loss remain in pixel space. On ImageNet-256, SpecSlot achieves a comparable final FID to a FLOPs-matched JiT control while converging faster. On ImageNet-512, it improves FID by 2.84 over the control with 3.9% fewer FLOPs, and comes within only 0.69 FID of JiT-B/16 at 38.2% lower cost. On text-to-image generation, SpecSlot surpasses JiT-B/8 on DPG-Bench by 1.06 at 40.6% lower forward cost.

[165] Wavelet Phase Diffusion for Structurally and Semantically Consistent Sim-to-Real Translation cs.AI | cs.CV | eess.IVPDF

Kaiwen Wang, Frank Bieder, Yinzhe Shen, Carlos Fernandez, Jan-Hendrik Pauls

TL;DR: 论文提出 Wavelet Phase Diffusion（ψ-PD），用于仿真到现实的图像/视频翻译，在保持结构与语义一致的同时缩小合成域与真实域的外观差距。方法在小波域进行相位扩散，并用低频随机化解耦合成光照先验，从而支持无配对开放域训练、低推理开销和实例级翻译。

Details

Motivation: 现有仿真到现实翻译方法难以同时保证真实感、结构/语义一致性和计算效率：条件控制方法模块昂贵，配对数据方法依赖复杂合成且易改变几何与语义，免训练编辑缺乏外观先验，Fourier 相位保留方法受全局频谱耦合影响而产生振铃、边界泄漏等伪影。

Result: 在 vKITTI→KITTI 图像翻译上，ψ-PD 在真实感和语义一致性上优于已有方法，并保持有竞争力的结构对齐；在 CARLA 视频翻译上接近配对数据方法的真实感，同时将 VLM 规划器的 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 5.4% 和 5.1%。

Insight: 创新点在于使用 Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform 的局部化小波包实现空间自适应相位注入，避免全局频谱干扰；并用低频随机化替换低频包以解耦合成光照先验。其空间局部性还支持实例级、区域级独立翻译，且训练无需配对数据、推理开销几乎可忽略。

Abstract: Simulation-to-reality translation must bridge the appearance gap between synthetic and real domains while preserving structural and semantic consistency. Conditioning-based methods achieve spatial alignment but introduce computationally expensive control modules. Paired-data methods achieve realism but rely on complex synthesis pipelines, often altering scene geometry and semantics. Training-free editing methods avoid both constraints but lack a learned appearance prior, limiting their perceptual quality. Recently proposed phase-preserving diffusion presents a promising alternative, but Fourier-domain formulations are constrained by global spectral coupling. This coupling induces spatial artifacts such as ringing and boundary leakage, thereby degrading structural and semantic consistency. We introduce Wavelet Phase Diffusion, which addresses this through two components. First, we operate in the Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform domain, whose localized wavelet packets enable spatially adaptive phase injection without global spectral interference. Second, Low-Frequency Randomization (LFR) replaces the low-frequency packet, decoupling the model from the synthetic illumination prior and enabling in-distribution real-world appearance. Both components train on unpaired open-domain data, and introduce negligible inference overhead. The spatial locality further enables instance-level translation, where individual objects or regions are translated to photorealistic appearance independently while the surrounding scene remains untranslated. On vKITTI $\to$ KITTI image translation, $ψ$-PD outperforms prior methods in realism and semantic consistency while maintaining competitive structural alignment. For CARLA video translation, $ψ$-PD approaches the realism of paired-data methods while reducing VLM planner ADE and FDE by $5.4%$ and $5.1%$, respectively.

TL;DR: 论文提出WISD（Weakening-Induced On-Policy Self-Distillation），一种面向大型推理模型的自蒸馏框架。该方法不依赖外部教师或特权信息，而是通过低比特量化或幅度剪枝构造原模型的“弱化版本”，利用原模型与弱化模型之间的对比作为on-policy训练信号，从而提升数学与代码推理能力。

Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法通常依赖特权信息来构造更强的自教师，当只有无标签提示可用时难以应用。论文希望在没有外部教师和特权信息的条件下，仍能通过自蒸馏提升推理模型。

Result: 在多种架构和规模的数学、代码推理基准上，量化与剪枝分别使平均准确率相比原模型提升4.0和5.5个百分点，并在无特权信息条件下达到SOTA水平。具体地，剪枝将Qwen3.5-4B在HMMT 2025上从57.7%提升至70.5%，在LiveCodeBench v6上从39.1%提升至56.7%；量化将Qwen3.6-35B-A3B在相同基准上分别从68.5%提升至74.6%、从61.8%提升至69.5%。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于反向利用“弱化”而非“强化”模型：通过低比特量化和幅度剪枝生成较弱副本，并将其与原模型的差异作为自蒸馏训练信号，避免了对特权信息的依赖。客观来看，该工作提供了一种无需外部教师、仅利用无标签提示即可进行on-policy自蒸馏的简洁框架，具有较强的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Self-distillation offers a way to improve reasoning models when a suitable external teacher is unavailable. However, common approaches rely on privileged information to construct a stronger self-teacher, limiting their use when only unlabeled prompts are available. In this paper, we introduce Weakening-Induced On-Policy Self-Distillation (WISD), which instead constructs a weaker counterpart of the original model and uses the contrast between the two as a training signal without requiring privileged information. We explore two ways to construct the weaker model: low-bit quantization and magnitude pruning. We evaluate WISD across diverse architectures and scales on mathematical and coding reasoning tasks. Across these benchmarks, quantization and pruning improve average accuracy over the original models by 4.0 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively, achieving state-of-the-art performance even without privileged information. Motivation: 当前LLM经RL后常在推理基准上提升，但这些提升可能被误读为真正推理能力；论文旨在区分任务特定的模式利用与真实推理，并揭示RL训练如何改变内部表征。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新包括：首次系统追踪小规模LLM在RL训练中的表征动态；将SAE特征发现扩展到有限逻辑域的假设驱动测试；并用因果干预证明RL后训练不增减概念信息，而是重组主导特征并将决策定位在后层状态。客观看，其价值在于用可控逻辑域和机制可解释性方法揭示“推理提升”可能混淆模式利用，为推理基准的效度提供警示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly described as acquiring “reasoning” skills after reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. However, whether these gains reflect genuine reasoning or more superficial adaptations remains underexplored. In this work, we probe LLMs trained in a finite and exhaustively analyzable logical domain, namely Tic-Tac-Toe, and trace how internal representations evolve across reinforcement learning (RL) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Quantitatively, RL yields high accuracy and robustness across prompt variations, suggesting the emergence of game reasoning. Mechanistic interpretability, however, paints a different picture: features extracted with sparse autoencoders (SAEs) reveal that models primarily adapt to rely more heavily on information already explicit in the prompt, such as whose turn it is, game progression and board occupancy. In comparison, high-level concepts like board symmetries and strategic forks remain weakly represented, raising concerns that reasoning benchmarks overestimate model abilities. This suggests that RL-driven “reasoning” may conflate task-specific adaptations to input information with reasoning ability. We provide, for the first time: (i) a systematic interpretability pipeline tracing representation dynamics across RL training in small-scale LLMs; (ii) an extension of SAE-based feature discovery to hypothesis-driven testing in a finite logical domain; and (iii) an interpretability-based demonstration that RL post-training neither adds nor removes concept information but reorganizes which features dominate the representation, with causal interventions locating the decision downstream of it, in the late-layer state.

[169] Proving and Improving the Platonic Representation Hypothesis with JEPAs cs.AI | cs.CV | stat.MLPDF

Randall Balestriero, Yann LeCun

TL;DR: 论文将柏拉图表示假设（PRH）形式化为定理：分别在不同模态上训练的JEPA，只要正样本对是同一场景的两个好视图且错位有界，就能学到仅差一个旋转的相同表示。作者进一步说明下一词元预测相当于使用坏视图的JEPA，因此语言模型与视觉对齐依赖规模；并用大规模图文数据验证对齐缩放律，证明好视图可显著提升样本效率与跨模态对齐。

Details

Motivation: PRH猜测不同模态模型随规模增大会收敛到同一世界表示，但原假设缺少证明和可操作配方；论文希望给出理论保证，并找到比单纯扩大规模更有效的跨模态表示对齐方法。

Result: 提出柏拉图表示定理并验证对齐缩放律；好视图达到同等对齐所需样本比下一词元预测少42倍；109M参数文本JEPA与DINOv2、CLIP图像编码器对齐优于评估的所有语言模型（至72B）及CLIP文本编码器；一个旋转即可将独立训练编码器变为图像描述器，COCO CIDEr 0.838；在无配对基准ImageNet-100 top-5上达64%，超过至8B语言模型的最高56%。

Insight: 创新点在于把PRH从猜想推进为可证明的JEPA定理，区分好视图与坏视图并解释下一词元预测的低效性，提出“视图质量而非仅规模”是构建柏拉图表示的关键杠杆；客观看，其价值在于理论、缩放实证和下游无配对对齐展示形成闭环。

Abstract: Whether models trained on different modalities converge to the same representation of the world is central to AI. The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) conjectures that they do as they scale, but without a proof it offers neither a guarantee nor a recipe. We turn the hypothesis into a theorem. First, we prove a \emph{Platonic Representation Theorem}: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) trained separately on any number of modalities learn the same representation up to one rotation whenever their positive pairs are two observations of one scene–\emph{good views}–and their misalignment is bounded otherwise. Second, we show that next-token prediction is a JEPA with \emph{bad views}, parts of a single document, which explains why language models align with vision only through scale. Third, we validate the theory from 145k captions to the texts of 940M web images, and uncover a scaling law of alignment that predicts unseen runs within seed noise. Good views need 42 times fewer training samples than next-token prediction on the same corpus to reach the same alignment, and a 109M-parameter text JEPA aligns with DINOv2 and CLIP’s image encoders better than every language model we evaluated, up to 72B parameters, and than CLIP’s text encoder: next-token prediction is a remarkably inefficient route to platonic representations. As a showcase, one rotation turns separately trained encoders into an image captioner (CIDEr .838 on COCO). Even that rotation needs no pair: on the latest pair-free benchmark, our text JEPA beats every zero-shot accuracy reported with language models up to 8B parameters, e.g., 64% against at most 56% top-5 on ImageNet-100. Positive views, and not scale alone, are thus the lever to build platonic representations.

[170] Training language models in simulation for psychiatric intake cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Samuel Schmidgall, Tim Strother, Alex Bijamov, Min Woo Sun, Anil Palepu

TL;DR: 论文研究在模拟环境中用多轮强化学习训练语言模型，以提升其结构化文本精神科接诊能力，覆盖病史采集、鉴别诊断和初始管理计划。通过精神科医生角色扮演标准化病人和独立评分面板进行随机双盲交叉评估，训练后的智能体在多类临床与沟通指标上显著优于同提示的基座模型，并在一组对抗性安全场景中降低急性风险漏分诊。

Details

Motivation: 精神科接诊访谈是重要诊断工具，需要医生建立关系并处理复杂重叠表现；论文希望利用可控模拟环境训练和压力测试精神科对话智能体，解决其在真实临床接诊中能力与安全性不足的问题。

Abstract: In psychiatric practice, the clinical intake interview remains a primary diagnostic instrument, requiring the clinician to establish rapport while disentangling complex, overlapping presentations. Here, we show that multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation environments improves language model performance on structured text-based psychiatric intake, spanning history-taking, differential diagnosis, and initial management planning. In a randomized, double-blind crossover study ($N = 60$ matched scenario pairs, 120 encounters) comparing the RL-trained agent against its identically prompted base model, 32 board-certified psychiatrists role-played standardized patients while a separate panel of 15 independent, board-certified psychiatrists scored the de-identified transcripts. The trained agent outperformed the base model in 76.7% of matched scenario pairs ($p < 0.0001$), with gains on an adapted clinical-examination rubric ($d_z = 0.77$, $p < 0.0001$), patient-centered communication (82.0% vs. 65.2%, $p < 0.001$), and differential diagnosis appropriateness (68.3% vs. 33.3%, $p < 0.001$). Across 127 psychiatrist-validated simulated intake scenarios, the trained agent nearly doubled its base model’s clinical rubric score (0.706 vs. 0.379; composite reward 0.564 vs. 0.294, $p < 0.0001$). To systematically probe conversational safety, we established two Delphi-consensus taxonomies, of clinical safety risks and relational competencies, from which 256 challenging adversarial presentations were derived; we find that training in simulation environments more than halved under-triage of acute safety risks (12.9% vs. 30.5%, $p < 0.0001$). Abstract: Financial AI workflows that reuse investor knowledge need persistent memory that integrates observations across sources and periods without losing identity or provenance. In a knowledge graph, recurring observations may use different surface forms, fragmenting relationship histories and preventing evidence from accumulating. We introduce Fusion and Identity Registry for Memory Evolution (FIRME), a registry-anchored memory graph that reconciles incoming observations to persistent canonical relationships and retains their source, time, and context. Within periods, rule-based fusion links investor-call discussions to portfolio cash-flow signals, directly or through a fixed product-topic ontology. Across periods, canonical identities and persistent aliases direct recurring observations to the same relationship history; deterministic updates summarize reinforcement, inactivity, and reactivation. We evaluate FIRME on proprietary investor-call and cash-flow records and on S&P 500 earnings-call transcripts. Compared with a historical registry that retains canonical identities and semantic fallback but omits alias memory, FIRME increases final-period Correct Match Rate by 7.8-12.1 percentage points across four dataset-backend settings under a fixed-judge and fallback-review protocol. We present VeriCUA, a platform-agnostic framework for verification through agentic exploration. [175] Evaluating Visual Realism of AI-Generated Videos with Eye Tracking cs.HC | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Result: 在最新生成模型视频上的两轮视觉图灵测试中，眼动模式与显式人工反馈具有一致性；提出的GazeEval在预测感知视觉真实感上达到71.3%准确率，表明眼动数据包含有效评估信息。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI-generated videos has so far relied on explicit human feedback, such as judgments or ratings. While these signals capture the conclusions viewers reach, they fail to offer insights into the perception that leads to them. This paper investigates eye tracking as a complementary human evaluation signal for AI-generated videos. We conducted two Visual Turing Test (VTT) experiments with a total of 90 participants on videos generated by latest generative models, in which participants watched each video twice and then gave their feedback. Across the experiments, we collected 4,286 valid eye-tracking trials on 438 videos. Our analysis indicates that gaze patterns offer informative evaluation signals that align with explicit human feedback. Building on the findings, we propose the first computational method GazeEval to predict the perceived visual realism of AI-generated videos from eye-tracking data. It achieves 71.3% accuracy in prediction, providing evidence that eye tracking data carries informative signals about how people evaluate a video. [176] Micro-Edits: Computational Support for Fine-Scale Edits in Film cs.HC | cs.CVPDF

Nhan Tran, Hijung Valentina Shin, Abe Davis, Mackenzie Leake

TL;DR: 论文关注电影剪辑中的细粒度微编辑（micro-edits），即通过调整片段精确时间或选择特定反应镜头来控制节奏与视觉注意力。作者先通过专业编辑的形成性研究理解其结构，进而提出数学形式化，并开发基于AI与优化的工具，帮助用户更简单高效地探索微编辑，最后通过多项用户研究和大量视频示例验证。

Details

Motivation: 标准剪辑工具中，细粒度微编辑需要用户将高层叙事目标手动映射到逐帧低层调整，过程缓慢繁琐；同时微编辑本身微妙且表达性强，不同选择对应不同创意目标，亟需计算支持。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量指标或基准对比，仅说明在多项用户研究和广泛视频示例中验证了工具的有效性，未宣称达到SOTA或与特定模型相当。

Insight: 创新点在于将电影微编辑结构进行形成性研究与数学形式化，并利用AI和优化辅助用户探索细粒度剪辑选择。客观来看，其贡献更偏向问题定义、交互工具设计和形式化建模，而非单一模型性能提升。

Abstract: In film editing, every frame matters. While macro, content-level edits shape the high-level narrative of a film, small local edits often control timing and direct visual attention. For example, editors can adjust the precise timing of clips to control the length of a meaningful silence, or cut to shots that highlight a specific character’s reactions to important moments. These small, detailed edits are slow and tedious to make in standard editing tools because users have to map high-level narrative goals to low-level frame-by-frame adjustments. At the same time, these fine-scale adjustments, which we call micro-edits, can be nuanced and expressive, with different choices serving different creative goals. In this work, we start by conducting a formative study with professional editors to understand the structure of micro-edits and how they are used. We then derive a mathematical formalization for this structure to develop tools that leverage AI and optimization to make exploring micro-edits simpler and more efficient for users. Finally, we validate our tools in multiple user studies and a wide range of video examples.
