[177] DuQUAD: A Dual-View Framework with Quality-Aware Evidence Pruning for Multi-Document Question Answering cs.IR | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yeonjun Choi, Seyeon Jeong, Jong Wook Kim, Beakcheol Jang

TL;DR: 论文提出 DuQUAD，一个面向多文档问答的双视角框架，通过局部智能体与全局智能体协同推理，并结合质量感知的证据剪枝来缓解 LLM 在长上下文中的 Lost in the Middle 问题。该方法利用结构融合分数引导局部证据恢复，同时用显式质量评分和句子级精炼过滤噪声证据。

Details

Motivation: 现有检索增强生成模型常忽略多文档上下文中部证据，文档重排序和注意力引导等方法在噪声场景下易受错误相关性估计和虚假文档影响，仍会掩盖有用证据。

Result: DuQUAD 在多个强 Lost-in-the-Middle 缓解基线（包括混合检索重排序、多智能体推理和上下文优化方法）上取得更优表现，平均准确率提升 3.0%，金标准证据召回率提升 9.4%，且计算成本显著低于现有多智能体框架。

Insight: 创新点在于将局部定向证据恢复与全局上下文理解结合，并通过结构融合分数、质量评分和句子级证据剪枝提升噪声鲁棒性；客观看，其可借鉴之处是双视角智能体协同与质量感知证据过滤的联合设计。

Abstract: Large language models with retrieval-augmented generation systematically underutilize evidence in the middle of multi-document contexts, a phenomenon known as Lost in the Middle. Existing mitigation strategies, such as document reordering and attention steering, can reduce boundary bias but remain brittle in noisy settings, where inaccurate relevance estimation and spurious documents still obscure useful evidence in central positions. We propose DuQUAD, a multi-document QA framework that mitigates positional bias through dual-view reasoning and quality-aware evidence pruning. DuQUAD combines a Local Agent guided by a Structural Fusion Score with a Global Agent that reasons over the full document set, providing both targeted evidence recovery and broad contextual understanding. The framework further filters candidate evidence using explicit quality scoring and refines it at the sentence level to reduce noise. DuQUAD consistently outperforms strong Lost-in-the-Middle mitigation baselines, including hybrid retrieval and reranking, multi-agent reasoning, and context optimization methods, improving accuracy by 3.0% on average over the strongest competing baseline and gold evidence recall by 9.4%, while substantially reducing computational cost compared to existing multi-agent frameworks.

[178] Relevance-Guided Residual Calibration for Spectral Compression of Dense Retrieval Embeddings cs.IR | cs.CLPDF

Yongkang Li, Panagiotis Eustratiadis, Evangelos Kanoulas

TL;DR: 论文针对稠密检索中谱压缩（spectral compression）的权重设定问题，提出了相关性引导残差校准（Relevance-Guided Residual Calibration, RRC）。该方法利用相关性标注，对每个保留的主方向按正负样本得分贡献的均值与方差估计基于间隔（margin）的权重，再把对数权重分解为幂律趋势项与残差项；随后将残差叠加到压缩器原有的对数权重上，既单独调整各方向，又保留整体频谱趋势。RRC 为闭式解，可直接用于 PCA、Whitening 和 SpecTemp，无需迭代训练或额外推理阶段。

Details

Motivation: 谱压缩把嵌入投影到较小的主子空间以降低存储与打分开销，但其由语料导出的加权规则把所有保留方向绑定到同一频谱趋势上，没有直接考虑各方向对相关性的个体贡献。因此，论文希望利用相关性判断来校正这些方向权重。

Result: 在 3 种编码器、9 个数据集（涵盖常规检索与推理密集型检索）上的实验显示：SpecTemp+RRC 在 BEIR 组和 BRIGHT 上，于每个目标维度都取得所测压缩器中最高的平均有效性；在 5 个 BRIGHT 数据集、768 维时，平均比 SpecTemp 提升 2.34 nDCG@10。该增益在标量量化与乘积量化下依然保持，改善了单个文档的存储与检索效果之间的权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于：用正负样本得分贡献的均值、方差构造基于间隔的方向权重；再通过“幂律趋势与残差”的分解，让相关性信号只调整各方向的偏差，而不破坏压缩器的整体频谱结构；同时以闭式解并入文档与查询共享的现有变换，无需训练或额外推理。可借鉴之处是这种“保留先验结构、只做残差校准”的轻量后处理思路。

Abstract: Spectral compression reduces the storage and scoring costs of dense retrieval by projecting embeddings onto a smaller principal subspace. However, its corpus-derived weighting rules tie all retained directions to a shared spectral trend, without directly accounting for their individual contributions to relevance. To address this, we propose Relevance-Guided Residual Calibration (RRC), a closed-form method for refining spectral weights using relevance judgments. RRC estimates margin-based weights from the means and variances of positive–negative score contributions along each retained direction. It retains directions with positive estimated weights, decomposes their log weights into a fitted power-law trend and a residual, and adds the residual to the compressor’s log weights on this support. This allows relevance judgments to adjust individual directions while preserving the compressor’s overall spectral trend. RRC applies to PCA, Whitening, and SpecTemp, and incorporates the corrections into the existing transformation shared by documents and queries, without iterative model training or an additional inference stage. Experiments with three encoders on nine datasets covering conventional and reasoning-intensive retrieval show that SpecTemp + RRC achieves the highest mean effectiveness among tested compressors on the BEIR group and BRIGHT at every target dimension. On the five evaluated BRIGHT datasets, it improves over SpecTemp by an average of 2.34 nDCG@10 points at 768 dimensions. The gains also persist under scalar and product quantization, improving the trade-off between per-document storage and retrieval effectiveness. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rrc-spectral-compression-B5CE/

cs.LG [Back]

[179] How Much Do Teacher Bridges Matter in Interleaved On-Policy Distillation? cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Leran Chen, Zhiyuan Yao, Lingnan Kong

TL;DR: 论文在交错式on-policy蒸馏中系统考察教师撰写的bridge片段是否以及如何起作用。以Qwen3-1.7B为学生、Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507为教师，在相同轨迹上比较对bridge token不计损失、前向KL和反向KL，并按token作者分离梯度。短训练和BF16推理下，未发现bridge作为上下文或训练目标带来准确率提升，三种损失统计等价；探索性收益未能复现，结论受BF16评估和停止生成问题限制。

Details

Motivation: On-policy蒸馏通常在学生生成回复上使用教师预测，一些方法插入教师撰写的bridge片段，但对这些token应否加损失及用前向还是反向KL存在分歧。论文旨在控制变量下量化teacher bridge的贡献并澄清训练策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B设置和预注册测试中，bridge仅占约2.7% token且梯度小、近正交，三种损失方向相似且在±5个百分点内统计等价；探索性定位bridge的收益在复现中未确认，四倍加权也无显著增益。

Insight: 创新在于按token作者分离梯度并在相同轨迹上比较三种bridge损失处理，结合预注册与复现来检验假设；客观价值主要是提供负结果和实验边界（BF16舍入、停止生成失败），提醒后续工作不能简单假定教师token监督普遍有效。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student using teacher predictions on student-generated responses. Some methods insert teacher-written segments, or bridges, but disagree on how to train on those tokens. We compare no loss, forward KL and reverse KL on identical trajectories using Qwen3-1.7B as student and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as teacher. We separate gradients by which model wrote each token. Under our short training runs and BF16 inference, pre-specified tests detected no added accuracy benefit from teacher text as context or as a training target. A one-paragraph bridge after the student’s first paragraph is only 2.7% of the tokens and contributes a small gradient nearly orthogonal to the student-token gradient. In this main comparison, the three loss treatments gave similar gradient directions and were statistically equivalent in accuracy within a +/-5 percentage-point margin. An exploratory pilot placing the bridge where failed responses disagreed most with the teacher suggested a benefit from teacher-token loss. A pre-specified replication did not confirm this, and fourfold weighting showed no significant accuracy benefit. Two limitations bound these findings. Among the tested amounts of training on fixed responses, gains peaked after four updates before the student increasingly failed to stop generating. None of the tested online training variants met the criteria for stable stopping and improved accuracy. Our evaluations used BF16 inference, which rounds away most of the small weight changes. When evaluated in exact FP32, the tested replication models frequently reached the response-length limit. Our accuracy findings therefore concern BF16 inference; they do not establish that teacher-token supervision is generally unnecessary.

[180] Persona Learning from Feedback for Personalized AI Assistants cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yiran Shen, Yu Xia, Liuyi Yao, Prithviraj Ammanabrolu

TL;DR: 论文提出 Persona Learning from Feedback（PLF）框架，用于个性化 AI 助手。该方法将可复用、由偏好反馈驱动的人格建模为共享冻结主干上的独立 LoRA 适配器，训练时利用冻结 self-teacher 结合人格规格与响应反馈提供 token 级监督，并用反馈门聚焦需改进的响应；部署时通过监督路由器根据请求或对话选择单个适配器或固定组合，无需用户特定微调。

Details

Motivation: 现有人格提示或基于人格条件生成微调的方法仅提供人格描述，难以明确特定回复中应改变或保留什么，以及何种行为适合当前请求。论文旨在从反馈中学习可复用且可自适应选择的助手人格。

Result: 论文在单轮个性化（模型生成批评）和多轮辅导（模拟学生反应）两种设置上评估 PLF。结果显示，PLF 在模型判断的偏好匹配度上优于固定人格策略以及人格提示、人格蒸馏等强基线，同时不降低通用能力。

Insight: 创新点在于将人格学习为独立的 LoRA 适配器，并利用响应特定反馈进行 token 级自监督校正；反馈门和部署时路由器使助手能按请求自适应选择或组合人格，且无需用户特定微调，提升了反馈训练行为的可复用性与自适应个性化能力。

Abstract: Personalizing AI assistants requires adapting their behavior to users’ preferences and task contexts. Existing work elicits assistant personas through persona prompting or finetuning on persona-conditioned generations. However, persona descriptions alone do not identify what to change or preserve in a particular response, or which behaviors suit the current request. We introduce $\textit{Persona Learning from Feedback (PLF)}$, a framework that learns reusable, preference-grounded assistant personas as independent LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone. During training, a frozen self-teacher conditions on persona specifications and response-specific feedback to provide token-level supervision on each adapter’s own generated prefixes. A feedback gate focuses corrective updates on responses judged to need improvement. At deployment, a supervised router uses the request or dialogue to select a single adapter or a fixed composition, without user-specific finetuning. We evaluate PLF on single-turn personalization with model-generated critiques and multi-turn tutoring with simulated students’ reactions. Across both settings, PLF improves model-judged preference fit over fixed-persona policies and strong baselines including persona prompting and persona distillation, demonstrating how feedback-trained behaviors can be reused for adaptive assistant personalization without degrading general capabilities.

[181] Contextual Embeddings Beyond the Gold Chunk cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.IRPDF

Sedigheh Eslami, Markus Krimmel, William Barber, Antoine Chaffin, Maximilian Schall

TL;DR: 论文提出 contextual embedding 模型 pplx-embed-v2-context，用于检索分布在多个 chunk 中的答案及验证答案所需的证据。方法通过从 query-aware context compression 模型中蒸馏 chunk 级细粒度相关性，摆脱对 gold chunk 标签的依赖，并采用视觉-语言骨干训练。作者还构建了两个新基准 Context-bench 和 Q2D-Chunk，并发布 9B 与 0.6B 模型。

Details

Motivation: 现有 contextual embedding 通常假设文档中只有一个相关 chunk，但真实检索场景需要找到跨多个 chunk 的答案以及用于验证答案的证据，且依赖 gold chunk 标签会限制模型与分块方式解耦。

Result: 9B 模型在 Context-bench、Q2D-Chunk 和 ConTEB 上取得新的 SOTA；在视觉检索基准 ViDoRe-V3 上，该模型在 dense retriever 中达到 SOTA，并且更细粒度的图像分块能带来性能提升。

Insight: 创新点在于从 query-aware context compression 模型蒸馏 graded chunk-level relevance，实现无需 gold-chunk 标签且与 chunking 无关的训练；同时利用视觉-语言骨干将检索能力迁移到图像检索，并推出标注答案 chunk 与验证证据的新基准和公开榜单。

Abstract: Contextual embeddings represent chunks of a document with respect to the document’s global context. These models are typically trained and evaluated under the assumption that a relevant document contains only a single relevant chunk. In this work, we argue that in real-world use cases a retriever should not only find answers spread over several chunks but also surface the evidence needed to verify them. To address this, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-context, a family of contextual embedding models trained by distilling graded chunk-level relevance from a query-aware context compression model. This removes the need for gold-chunk labels and makes the training targets independent of the chunking. We utilize a vision-language backbone for training and release pplx-embed-v2-context in 9B and 0.6B sizes with compatible embedding spaces. On the evaluation side, we introduce two benchmarks that label both the chunks that answer a query and the evidence needed to verify the answer. Context-bench is a controlled diagnostic, and Q2D-Chunk is built from real-world search queries. To encourage further research, we also publish Q2D-Chunk as a public leaderboard. Our 9B model sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks and ConTEB. We find that the visual retrieval capabilities of our 9B model transfer from its vision-language base model and propose to extend chunking to image inputs. We demonstrate that our models achieve state-of-the-art performance among dense retrievers on visual ViDoRe-V3 and that performance improves with granular image chunking.

[182] A Novel Approach to Evaluating Evaluation Metrics for Multi-Output Structured Prediction cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Akshay Vyas, Angelo Pimienta, Nicholas Ruozzi

TL;DR: 论文研究多输出结构化预测中单一真实标签难以可靠评价的问题，提出人类评估者会依赖任务特定不变量（如着色图像中的目标边界、翻译中的命名实体）判断输出是否可接受。作者用近似任务特定不变量作为诊断工具，系统评估视觉和语言任务中的现有指标，并通过增强损失在模型输出中鼓励这些不变量，以检验指标可靠性。

Details

Motivation: 多输出结构化预测可能存在多个同样有效的输出，而训练数据通常只提供一个真实标签，导致现有评价指标难以可靠判断模型输出质量。论文希望从任务特定不变量角度揭示标准指标何时失效。

Result: 实验表明，通过增强损失强制满足不变量后，常用分布型指标有显著提升，而传统指标仅轻微变化；该不变量驱动评估还暴露了标准指标无法检测有意义差异的情况，并刻画了分布型指标成功或仍不足的条件。

Insight: 创新点在于将任务特定不变量引入评价指标评估，用其作为诊断工具并通过增强损失系统性测试指标可靠性；客观上，这为多输出结构化预测的评价提供了可解释、可干预的分析视角。

Abstract: In multi-output structured prediction tasks, while only one ground truth label may be provided in the training data, multiple equally valid outputs may be possible, making reliable evaluation a persistent challenge. We postulate that human evaluators implicitly use task-specific invariants, e.g., object boundaries in colorized images or named entities in translations, to judge if an output is acceptable. Under this assumption, we introduce a notion of approximate task-specific invariants and use them as diagnostic tools to evaluate a variety of existing metrics for vision and language tasks. We use these task invariants as part of a framework to systematically test metric reliability by encouraging domain-relevant invariants in model outputs via an augmented loss function. In our experiments, we observe that enforcing invariants with an augmented loss yields substantial improvements in popular distributional metrics while more traditional metrics change only marginally. Through this invariants-driven evaluation, we expose where standard metrics fail to detect meaningful differences, and we highlight the conditions under which distributional metrics succeed or still fall short.

[183] Event-SAMA: Language-Grounded Video Object Segmentation on Neuromorphic Event Streams cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Sergei Fedorchenko, Max Zehnder, Nikola Zubić, Rong Zou, Davide Scaramuzza

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Event-SAMA-239K，这是将 SAMA-239K 视频对话语料及全部 SAMA 训练数据（含 30,650 个视频片段和 392,162 张图像）通过光流、FILM 或扫视上采样加线性事件生成器转换为合成事件流的数据集，并完整保留掩码、指代表达和对话标注；同时提出 Event-SAMA，将 SAMA-4B 适配到事件域，采用由预训练 RGB 权重初始化的双通道极性 patch embedding 和零初始化的 event adapter，建立了首个事件原生指代视频目标分割（RVOS）基线。

Details

Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和无运动模糊等优势，但指代视频目标分割（RVOS）领域缺乏事件流数据和模型。论文旨在填补这一空白，构建事件域 RVOS 数据集并给出首个事件原生基线。

Result: Event-SAMA 端到端训练后在 MeViS valid_u 上取得 24.37 J&F，在转换的 ReVOS valid 子集上取得 22.33 J&F，成为首个事件原生 RVOS 基线（RGB SAMA-4B 在 MeViS 上为 55.4，说明仍存较大差距）。冻结的 RGB SAMA-4B 用伪 RGB 渲染或 E2VID 重建仅得 13.23/15.94 J&F；消融显示多源训练数据（+4.99）、RGB 均值 event-stem 初始化（+4.33）和可训练 SAM2 掩码头（+2.76）有效。

Insight: 创新点在于：通过光流/FILM 上采样加线性事件生成器的合成转换方式，低成本构建保留全部标注的事件流 RVOS 数据集；以及从预训练 RGB 权重初始化双通道极性 patch embedding、配合零初始化 event adapter 将 RGB 模型迁移到事件域，使模型无需重训即可在真实事件相机录制上工作。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range and no motion blur, but referring video object segmentation (RVOS) lacks event-stream datasets and models. We introduce Event-SAMA-239K, a synthetic-event conversion of the SAMA-239K grounded video-chat corpus and all additional SAMA training data: 30,650 video clips and 392,162 images. Optical-flow, FILM or saccade upsampling followed by a linear event generator preserves all mask, referring-expression and dialogue annotations. We also introduce Event-SAMA, adapting SAMA-4B with a two-channel polarity patch embedding initialised from pretrained RGB weights and a zero-initialised event adapter. End-to-end training yields 24.37 $\mathcal{J}&\mathcal{F}$ on MeViS valid_u and 22.33 $\mathcal{J}&\mathcal{F}$ on the converted ReVOS valid subset, establishing the first event-native RVOS baseline (RGB SAMA-4B: 55.4 on MeViS). Frozen RGB SAMA-4B with pseudo-RGB renderings or E2VID reconstructions of the same events scores 13.23 and 15.94 $\mathcal{J}&\mathcal{F}$. Without retraining, Event-SAMA works on real event-camera recordings when the accumulation window matches their event density. A seed-controlled ablation with a measured noise floor of 0.47 $\mathcal{J}&\mathcal{F}$ finds gains from diverse training sources over MeViS alone (+4.99), RGB-mean event-stem initialisation (+4.33), and a trainable SAM2 mask decoder (+2.76); the event adapter, partial ViT unfreezing and event polarity are within noise at this budget. An earlier, partially converted corpus snapshot trained with the identical recipe scores 1.38 $\mathcal{J}&\mathcal{F}$ higher on MeViS and 1.12 higher on ReVOS. Both are single runs that also differ in schedule length, so we draw no conclusion about corpus scale.

[184] Quantum-Inspired Convolutional Networks for High-Resolution Pseudo-Label Generation from Multimodal Earth Observation Data cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Abhishek Singh, Giovanni Cuozzo, Alexander Jacob, Claudia Notarnicola

TL;DR: 论文提出一种量子启发的卷积编码器-解码器网络，利用Sentinel-1 SAR与Sentinel-2多光谱影像，在仅有粗分辨率MODIS土地覆盖图引导下生成高分辨率伪标签。框架先通过重建分支进行自监督多模态表示学习，再结合低分辨率标签监督，从异构遥感输入中推断细尺度语义结构。

[186] Rethinking Neural Thickets Without Weight Perturbations cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Nicolas Dufour, Elouan Gardès, Amil Dravid, Patrick Pérez, Loic Landrieu

TL;DR: 论文重新审视了 Neural Thickets 假设，发现随机权重扰动的性能增益并非源于权重空间的几何特性，而是来自输出多样性、基于验证集的选择和投票。作者提出 Perturb-Select-Vote (PSV) 框架，并证明仅对输出 logits 添加随机偏置（完全冻结模型）即可匹配甚至超过全权重扰动集成。该结论在语言推理任务和视觉领域偏移任务（包括地球观测）上得到验证。

Details

Motivation: 论文质疑 Neural Thickets 假设：随机高斯权重扰动带来的提升是否真的因为模型处于权重空间中的密集专家邻域？出发点是寻找更简单、无需训练且更通用的解释。

Result: 论文复现了原始增益，但发现仅扰动单个解码块或仅对 logits 加随机偏置就能匹配或超过全权重扰动。在大多数推理任务上，Logit PSV 达到或超过全权重扰动的水平；在视觉领域偏移（包括地球观测）上显著提升性能，而全权重扰动额外收益甚微。

Insight: 创新点在于将 Neural Thickets 重新解释为输出多样性、选择和投票的推理时方法（PSV），并证明小权重扰动主要通过诱导 logit 偏移起作用。客观分析：论文揭示了简单随机 logit 偏置加验证选择与投票的强大效果，为集成方法提供了新视角，并降低了计算成本（无需扰动权重），具有实用价值。

Abstract: The Neural Thickets hypothesis posits that pretrained language models lie in dense neighborhoods of task-specific experts in weight space, which random Gaussian perturbations can uncover. We reproduce the reported gains, but show that they do not require this geometric explanation. Perturbing a single decoder block already recovers much of the improvement, while keeping the model entirely frozen and adding a random bias to its output logits matches or exceeds full-weight perturbations on most reasoning tasks. This suggests an alternative explanation based on output diversity, validation-based selection, and voting. We formalize this view as Perturb-Select-Vote (PSV), a general procedure that generates perturbed predictors, selects high-performing members on a small validation set, and aggregates their predictions. This interpretation connects Neural Thickets to established inference-time methods, including sampling, self-consistency, and verifier-guided selection. A first-order analysis further shows that small weight perturbations act primarily through their induced logit shifts. We extend this analysis to vision, where logit PSV substantially improves performance under domain shift, including on Earth observation, while full-weight perturbations provide little additional benefit. Together, these results suggest that the gains attributed to nearby weight-space experts can instead be obtained through output diversity, selection, and voting. Logit PSV realizes these benefits with a fully frozen model, providing a simple, training-free alternative to full-weight perturbation ensembles across language and vision.

Nhan Trong Luu, Duong Trung Luu, Thang Cong Truong

TL;DR: 论文研究脉冲神经网络中的群等变性，指出LIF神经元的膜递归会破坏G等变卷积的等变性，并给出神经元参数在群作用联合轨道上保持恒定的充要条件。基于此提出逐通道参数化的Group-Coupled LIF（GC-LIF），与p4/p4m等变卷积和脉冲率群最大池化组成SGE-Net，实现无需旋转数据增强的旋转不变脉冲视觉模型。

Details

Motivation: SNN在视觉任务中通常只能通过数据增强获得旋转不变性，而现有群等变CNN难以直接移植到SNN，因为LIF的膜状态递归可能破坏等变性，论文旨在为脉冲网络建立忠实的群等变结构。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) promise event-driven, low-power inference, but on vision tasks they still acquire rotation invariance only through data augmentation. Group-equivariant CNNs build such symmetries into the architecture, yet a faithful spiking analogue is missing: a leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neuron placed after a $G$-equivariant convolution can break equivariance through its membrane recurrence. We give a necessary and sufficient condition for this not to happen. The layer is equivariant at every time step if and only if the neuron parameters $(β, v_{\mathrm{th}})$ are constant along the joint orbits of the group action on the (group, space) index. We solve it in closed form, and it covers any elementwise stateful neuron. Its sufficiency half specialises a known result for equivariant recurrent networks; its necessity half rules out the heterogeneous per-neuron parameters common in SNNs. The per-channel parameterisation that satisfies it, Group-Coupled LIF (GC-LIF), is combined with $p4$ and $p4m$-equivariant convolutions and a spike-rate group max-pool into SGE-Net. On MNIST, KMNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, evaluating each checkpoint on the clean test set and all its group-transformed copies, SGE-Net trained without rotations is invariant under $C_4$ and $D_4$ and outperforms a plain SNN trained with rotations in nine of ten dataset-group pairs, whereas non-equivariant baselines lose up to $72$ percentage points in the worst case. Operator-level tests confirm the theory before and after training, and an analytical estimate places SGE-Net at $1.7\times$ the arithmetic energy of a standard CNN and $2.3\times$ below its ANN counterpart at eight time steps.

At the core of LaWAM is a latent-action-conditioned Latent World Model (LaWM). Details

Motivation: 现有具身指令跟随规划器要么依赖大规模标注轨迹，要么使用少样本/零样本LLM提示一次性串行生成完整计划；这类方法依赖语言常识、过早承诺初始计划且通常只在失败后更新，导致鲁棒性和状态感知较弱。

Result: 在ALFRED基准的零样本评估中，AtomTree优于先前的基于LLM的规划器；摘要未给出具体数值，但宣称在同类LLM规划方法中表现更优。

Abstract: Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. TL;DR: 论文指出机器人抓取可供性与轨迹预测本质上是连续几何预测问题，而现有视觉语言模型将其转化为坐标文本的token自回归生成，导致空间结构丢失、精度截断和误差累积。作者发现0.5-2B参数的紧凑VLM中间隐藏状态已包含丰富空间信息，因此提出在冻结骨干网络上附加轻量、几何兼容的预测头，通过指令条件化查询和视觉token交叉注意力直接回归可供性框与轨迹路点。

Motivation: 现有VLM将空间坐标token化并自回归解码，忽略空间结构且截断连续精度，解码过程中坐标token错误会累积；论文旨在避免这种语言生成式几何预测，探索紧凑VLM隐藏状态中的空间信息并高效解码。

Abstract: Predicting where to grasp and how to move is fundamentally a geometry problem. Vision-language models treat it as language generation instead. They tokenize spatial coordinates into text and decode them autoregressively. This mechanism ignores spatial structure and truncates continuous precision. Errors compound across coordinate tokens during decoding. In this paper, we show that compact off-the-shelf VLMs with 0.5-2B parameters already encode rich spatial information in their intermediate hidden states. Autoregressive decoding is the dominant bottleneck for trajectory prediction, while affordance prediction depends additionally on feature depth and decoder architecture. We expose the latent spatial information with a lightweight, geometry-compatible prediction head that attaches to a frozen backbone. It reads features from an intermediate hidden layer, conditions a learned query on the task instruction and refines it via cross-attention over the visual tokens to regress affordance boxes and trajectory waypoints directly as continuous values, without modifying the backbone. These results demonstrate a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for instruction-conditioned 2D geometric prediction.

Motivation: 推理型VLA先写推理轨迹再解码动作，错误推理会导致错误动作，甚至看似正确的推理也可能误导规划。现有工作主要判断推理是否正确，本文则关注推理应以多强程度影响动作，即如何避免过度信任推理。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) policies write a reasoning trace and then decode the action from it. A wrong trace yields a wrong action, and even a correct-looking trace can mislead the plan. Existing remedies ask whether the reasoning is right. We ask how strongly it should steer. CoC-CFG applies classifier-free guidance between the policy’s context and the same context without the reasoning span. One weight per Euler step attenuates or amplifies the reasoning, and w=1 recovers the original policy. We treat the choice of this weight as a Markov decision process whose action values are measured, not bootstrapped. With the expert frozen and the noise fixed, applying a weight for one step and finishing the plan under the current controller returns that weight’s value exactly. Q-adaptive guidance fits these values with small critics and improves the controller conservatively. In the Alpasim driving simulator it outperforms every fixed weight and online RL (SAC, GRPO) in open loop, and in closed loop it passes 262 of 300 scenes, against 252 for the original policy. On held-out frames of the Waymo end-to-end driving benchmark, its first three stages outperform every fixed weight, four adaptive-guidance methods, SAC and GRPO. On the hidden test set, a frozen Alpamayo 1.5 with rule-based camera and vehicle conversions and Q-adaptive guidance sets a new state of the art among published methods: 8.135159 rater feedback score, against 8.079 for the best published method.

Abstract: Monocular RGB tracking is useful for robots operating near people, but perception errors affect binary stop/proceed decisions in ways that geometric accuracy metrics do not capture. We examine how much decision error arises from the safety rule versus perception. Abstract: As robots and intelligent systems increasingly interact with humans, they must anticipate long sequences of future actions from only brief observations. While purely neural models typically require extensive observations to predict future actions and remain difficult to interpret and adjust, symbolic priors offer a structured alternative. To this end, we introduce SymAnt, a novel neuro-symbolic approach that unifies the expressiveness of neural networks with the structured reasoning of scene and knowledge graphs. Given a short video, SymAnt performs a joint-search over both graphs to construct a symbolic context, enabling unified reasoning over spatial relationships, object attributes, affordances, and associated actions. This symbolic context serves as a prior for an iterative refinement process that progressively generates and improves the sequence of future actions. By grounding predictions in symbolic context, SymAnt anticipates long-horizon actions from short observation windows in a parameter-efficient manner, while enabling users to directly edit the underlying knowledge to adjust predictions without further training. Through our experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SymAnt, particularly when only short observation contexts are available, across five diverse datasets: Breakfast, 50 Salads, EPIC-KITCHENS, EGTEA Gaze+, and Assembly101.

We close this gap with V33DA, a dataset-centered multimodal benchmark for spatial vocal attribution in social zebra finches: it comprises 33,625 verified events coupling 5-channel audio with three camera views, calibrated 3D pose for every visible candidate, and per-bird radio telemetry across 10 individuals and three experiments. Abstract: To succeed in real-world long-horizon software development, coding agents must satisfy multi-dimensional task specifications, such as interface compatibility and error handling, and do so without breaking existing dependencies. However, current works lack specification in both quantity and diversity, often emphasizing primary goals while ignoring complex constraints. Synthesizing tasks to bridge this gap presents two challenges: naively adding specifications risks reducing tasks to trivial checklists rather than compelling joint reasoning, and verifying these subtle interactions risks overlooking hidden flaws or rejecting valid alternatives. To address these challenges, we introduce CodeSpecWeave, a framework for synthesizing tasks with rich, interdependent specifications. Details

Abstract: Myocardial Infarction (MI) is a severe and potentially fatal cardiac condition caused by myocardial injury due to ischemia, and timely detection can substantially reduce its mortality. In this work, we propose Visionary V2, a multi-step, adaptive filtration method for ECG signal normalization, and use it to study machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches to MI detection on the PTB Diagnostic ECG Database using all 15 available leads. [200] From the Superderivative to a Relative-Gradient Edge Operator eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Alejandro Garnung Menéndez

TL;DR: 论文从乘法导数（superderivative）出发，重新审视对数梯度幅值这一相对梯度边缘算子，构造度量相对灰度变化、对全局灰度缩放不变的边缘强度图。方法可用经典梯度核加点除两步实现，并在合成图与BSDS500上同Sobel等一阶导数滤波及Canny比较。作者强调贡献在于算子构造及其不变性，而非新的SOTA。

Details

Motivation: Sobel、Prewitt、Roberts、Scharr等一阶导数边缘检测器度量绝对灰度差，缺乏对全局强度缩放的不变性；论文希望借助superderivative与对数梯度的类比，得到度量相对变化且尺度不变的边缘算子。

Result: 在合成真值边缘和BSDS500上，绝对梯度与对数梯度之间的参数族中，小中间指数表现最好：按单图指标略优于经典核且统计可检测；在与单σ Canny相同高斯平滑下，ODS持平、OIS和AP领先，但调参Canny在ODS仍更优。单阈值优势在留出测试集未复现，全相对端点从未最佳，整体并非SOTA。

Insight: 创新点是将乘法导数/superderivative与对数梯度幅值联系起来，提出局部、无原点、各向同性的相对梯度边缘算子，获得全局灰度缩放不变性；实现仅需经典梯度核加点除，并给出在绝对梯度与对数梯度间插值的参数族。客观看，主要价值是构造与不变性分析，而非性能突破。

Abstract: First-derivative edge detectors such as Sobel, Prewitt, Roberts and Scharr measure absolute grey-level differences. We revisit the log-gradient magnitude, a relative-gradient edge operator, and motivate it by analogy with the superderivative, a multiplicative derivative in which the independent variable is perturbed multiplicatively rather than additively. In one and two dimensions the logarithm of the superderivative is a radial logarithmic derivative; its local, origin-free, isotropic analogue is the magnitude of the logarithmic gradient. The resulting operator measures relative rather than absolute change, giving an edge-strength map that is invariant under global intensity rescaling. We present the theory, show how the operator can be implemented in two steps—a classical gradient kernel plus a pointwise division—and compare it with standard first-derivative filters on synthetic images with ground-truth edges and on the official BSDS500 benchmark. We also study a parametric family that interpolates between the absolute gradient and the log-gradient; empirically, the best members are small intermediate exponents, which edge the classical kernels by a small but statistically detectable margin in the per-image metric and, with the same Gaussian smoothing as the single-$σ$ Canny, match it in ODS and lead it in OIS and AP, while the tuned Canny remains ahead in ODS. The single-threshold margin over the kernels does not replicate on the held-out test split, and the fully relative endpoint is never the best member, its ranking depending on the edge-map normalisation. The contribution is the construction and the invariance it buys, not a new state of the art.

[201] Blind Video Denoising for Vocal Tract rtMRI using a Self-Supervised Ensemble eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Julian Andres Hernandez-Potes, Tomás Arias-Vergara, Daiqi Liu, Andreas Maier, Paula Andrea Perez-Toro

TL;DR: 论文面向声道实时磁共振成像（rtMRI）视频，提出一种自监督集成去噪框架：将时空超分辨率学习与由方差稳定非局部均值滤波得到的伪监督结合，并用双模型融合平衡噪声抑制与高频声道结构保留。

Details

Motivation: rtMRI需要在时间分辨率与信号质量之间权衡，且运动声道无法获取干净参考，监督去噪不适用；目标是在无干净参考条件下抑制噪声并保留精细发音结构。

Result: 在USC-75多说话人自然噪声数据上，对未见受试者SNR提升11.9 dB且保留更多高频发音内容；在严重高斯、瑞利和莱斯噪声下PSNR提升7.6–11.8 dB，SSIM约提升三倍。与各向异性扩散、块匹配滤波、非局部均值等调优方法相比，同等去噪强度下所提方法更好保持高频与结构保真。

Insight: 创新点在于自监督集成去噪、方差稳定非局部均值伪监督及双模型融合；消融表明方差稳定伪监督（尤其硬阈值）对去噪至关重要，为无干净参考的动态医学影像去噪提供了可借鉴思路。

Abstract: Real-time magnetic resonance imaging of the vocal tract makes speech articulation directly observable, but the trade-off between temporal resolution and signal quality introduces substantial noise and obscures fine articulatory detail. Since clean references cannot be acquired for a moving vocal tract, supervised denoising approaches are not applicable in this setting. We propose a self-supervised denoising framework that combines spatiotemporal super-resolution learning with pseudo-supervision derived from a variance-stabilized non-local means filter. A dual-model fusion strategy balances noise suppression and preservation of high-frequency articulatory structure. Experiments on the USC-75 multi-speaker dataset under natural noise conditions show that the ensemble raises the signal-to-noise ratio by 11.9 dB on unseen subjects while attaining more high-frequency articulatory content in the frames. Among tuned implementations of anisotropic diffusion, block-matching filtering, non-local means and pre-smoothed non-local means, every configuration that achieves comparable noise suppression does so at the cost of high-frequency content or of structural fidelity. Under severe added Gaussian, Rayleigh and Rician noise, peak signal-to-noise ratio improves by 7.6 to 11.8 dB and structural similarity roughly triples. An ablation shows that the variance-stabilized pseudo-supervision is an essential element for denoising, and that the effect is attributable to the hard thresholding contained in the stabilization transform.

[202] Agentic LLM reasoning in pediatric diabetes outcome prediction from multimodal medical records q-bio.QM | cs.CLPDF

Daniel Palacios, Guantong Qi, Anna Kadi, Natali Borrego, Sukru Aras

TL;DR: 论文提出CEDAR，一种证据引用与自我审计的LLM智能体流程，利用多模态儿科糖尿病医疗记录（结构化数据、笔记衍生的社会决定因素及T1D的连续血糖监测数据）预测24个月后的结局。在2,489名T2D和4,018名T1D青少年队列中，CEDAR在多个结局预测上显著优于传统机器学习模型，并展现出高引证准确性和临床可解释性。

Details

Motivation: LLM智能体虽被用于临床预测，但尚未在儿科验证；儿科队列规模小、结局罕见，传统机器学习可能受限，需要可解释且可靠的预测方法。

Result: CEDAR在T2D中汇总AUC为0.815（对比ML的0.656-0.700），T1D中为0.756（对比0.603-0.631），在稀疏筛查结局上提升最明显；其引用的70,246个数值中仅4个与病历不符，两名儿科内分泌学家仅阅读其证据和推理后约90%病例预测一致，专家间一致率90.5%。

Insight: 创新点在于将LLM智能体设计为证据引用和自我审计的临床预测管道，融合多模态记录并输出可追溯推理；客观看，其价值在于提升小样本罕见结局的预测性能与临床可信度，通过专家一致性验证了可解释性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are proposed for clinical prediction but are untested in pediatrics, where cohorts are small and outcomes are uncommon. Using records from 2,489 youth with type 2 diabetes (T2D) and 4,018 with type 1 diabetes (T1D), spanning structured data, note-derived social determinants and, in T1D, continuous glucose monitoring data, we predicted seven T2D outcomes, four also in T1D, at 24 months from 18 months of follow-up. CEDAR (Clinical Evidence-Driven Agent for Risk-prediction), an evidence-citing, self-auditing LLM pipeline, outperformed trained machine learning in both cohorts (pooled area under the receiver operating characteristic curve 0.815 versus 0.656 to 0.700 in T2D and 0.756 versus 0.603 to 0.631 in T1D), most on the sparsely screened outcomes. Only 4 of 70,246 values it cited did not match the chart. Reading only the evidence and reasoning cited by CEDAR, two pediatric endocrinologists reached the same prediction as CEDAR in about nine of ten cases, agreeing with each other on 90.5%.
